Implement the following plan:

# ENT-64: Status Shows Session Information

## Overview

Enhance `entire status` to show active sessions grouped by worktree, including first prompt, timestamp, checkpoint count, and uncheckpointed work indicator.

## Target Output

```
Enabled (manual-commit)

Active Sessions:
  /Users/alex/workspace/cli (main)
    abc-123  "Fix auth bug in login..."   2m ago   3 checkpoints
    def-456  "Add dark mode support..."   15m ago  1 checkpoint (uncheckpointed changes)

  /Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/3 (alex/ent-64-status-shows-sessions)
    ghi-789  "Refactor status cmd..."     5m ago   0 checkpoints (uncheckpointed changes)
```

No active sessions → just show existing one-liner (`Enabled (manual-commit)`).

## Implementation Steps

### Step 1: Add `FirstPrompt` field to session state types

Both types must stay in sync (they're independent but structurally mirrored).

**Files:**
- `cmd/entire/cli/session/state.go` — Add `FirstPrompt string` field to `State` struct (~line 73, after `TranscriptPath`)
- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_types.go` — Add `FirstPrompt string` field to `SessionState` struct (after `TranscriptPath`)

### Step 2: Thread user prompt through `InitializeSession`

**`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/strategy.go`** (~line 413):
- Add `userPrompt string` parameter to `SessionInitializer.InitializeSession()` signature

**`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go`** (~line 786):
- Update `ManualCommitStrategy.InitializeSession()` signature to accept `userPrompt string`
- Pass it to `initializeSession()`

**`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_session.go`** (~line 196):
- Update `initializeSession()` to accept `userPrompt string`
- Store truncated prompt (first ~100 chars) in `state.FirstPrompt`
- Use `strategy.TruncateDescription()` (already exists in `common.go`) for truncation

**`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/auto_commit.go`** (~line 906):
- Update `AutoCommitStrategy.InitializeSession()` signature and store the prompt

**`cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`** (~line 78-81):
- Pass `hookData.input.UserPrompt` to `initializer.InitializeSession()`

**`cmd/entire/cli/hooks_geminicli_handlers.go`** (~line 478-481):
- Pass `input.UserPrompt` to `initializer.InitializeSession()`

**Backfill on resume:** In `InitializeSession` where `state != nil` (session already exists, ~line 804), backfill `FirstPrompt` if empty (same pattern as `AgentType` backfill on line 808).

### Step 3: Add relative time helper

**`cmd/entire/cli/setup.go`** (or a small helper near the status code):
- Add `timeAgo(t time.Time) string` — simple relative time formatter
- Cases: "just now" (<1m), "Xm ago", "Xh ago", "Xd ago"
- No external dependencies (don't import go-humanize for this)

### Step 4: Enhance `entire status` to show active sessions

**`cmd/entire/cli/setup.go`**:

Modify `runStatus()` to call a new `writeActiveSessions(w)` function after the settings line.

New function `writeActiveSessions(w io.Writer)`:
1. Create `session.NewStateStore()`
2. Call `store.List(ctx)` to get all session states
3. Filter to active only (`EndedAt == nil`)
4. Group by `WorktreePath`
5. For each unique worktree path, resolve branch name via `git -C <path> rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD`
6. Sort groups (main worktree first, then alphabetical)
7. Sort sessions within each group by `StartedAt` (newest first)
8. Format and write output — worktree header shows `<path> (<branch>)`

Per session line: `{sessionID-short}  "{firstPrompt}..."  {timeAgo}  {N} checkpoint(s) [(uncheckpointed changes)]`

- Session ID: truncate to reasonable length for display
- First prompt: use `FirstPrompt` from state, fallback to `"(unknown)"` for old sessions
- Uncheckpointed changes: show when `PendingPromptAttribution != nil`

### Step 5: Tests

- **Unit test for `timeAgo`** — verify formatting for various durations
- **Unit test for `writeActiveSessions`** — create mock state store with test session states, verify output formatting
- **Update existing `InitializeSession` tests** — add `userPrompt` parameter to test calls
- **Test backfill behavior** — verify `FirstPrompt` gets populated on session resume

    24→// State represents the state of an active session.
    25→// This is stored in .git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json
    26→type State struct {
    27→	// SessionID is the unique session identifier
    28→	SessionID string `json:"session_id"`
    29→
    30→	// BaseCommit is the HEAD commit when the session started
    31→	BaseCommit string `json:"base_commit"`
    32→
    33→	// WorktreePath is the absolute path to the worktree root
    34→	WorktreePath string `json:"worktree_path,omitempty"`
    35→
    36→	// WorktreeID is the internal git worktree identifier (empty for main worktree)
    37→	// Derived from .git/worktrees/<name>/, stable across git worktree move
    38→	WorktreeID string `json:"worktree_id,omitempty"`
    39→
    40→	// StartedAt is when the session was started
    41→	StartedAt time.Time `json:"started_at"`
    42→
    43→	// EndedAt is when the session was explicitly closed by the user.
    44→	// nil means the session is still active or was not cleanly closed.
    45→	EndedAt *time.Time `json:"ended_at,omitempty"`
    46→
    47→	// CheckpointCount is the number of checkpoints created in this session
    48→	CheckpointCount int `json:"checkpoint_count"`
    49→
    50→	// CondensedTranscriptLines tracks lines already included in previous condensation
    51→	CondensedTranscriptLines int `json:"condensed_transcript_lines,omitempty"`
    52→
    53→	// UntrackedFilesAtStart tracks files that existed at session start (to preserve during rewind)
    54→	UntrackedFilesAtStart []string `json:"untracked_files_at_start,omitempty"`
    55→
    56→	// FilesTouched tracks files modified/created/deleted during this session
    57→	FilesTouched []string `json:"files_touched,omitempty"`
    58→
    59→	// LastCheckpointID is the checkpoint ID from last condensation, reused for subsequent commits without new content
    60→	LastCheckpointID id.CheckpointID `json:"last_checkpoint_id,omitempty"`
    61→

    63→	AgentType agent.AgentType `json:"agent_type,omitempty"`
    64→
    65→	// Token usage tracking (accumulated across all checkpoints in this session)

    68→	// Transcript position when session started (for multi-session checkpoints)

    75→	// PromptAttributions tracks user and agent line changes at each prompt start.

    83→
    84→// PromptAttribution captures line-level attribution data at the start of each prompt.
    85→// By recording what changed since the last checkpoint BEFORE the agent works,
    86→// we can accurately separate user edits from agent contributions.
    87→type PromptAttribution struct {
    88→	// CheckpointNumber is which checkpoint this was recorded before (1-indexed)
    89→	CheckpointNumber int `json:"checkpoint_number"`
    90→
    91→	// UserLinesAdded is lines added by user since the last checkpoint
    92→	UserLinesAdded int `json:"user_lines_added"`
    93→
    94→	// UserLinesRemoved is lines removed by user since the last checkpoint
    95→	UserLinesRemoved int `json:"user_lines_removed"`
    96→
    97→	// AgentLinesAdded is total agent lines added so far (base → last checkpoint).
    98→	// Always 0 for checkpoint 1 since there's no previous checkpoint to measure against.
    99→	AgentLinesAdded int `json:"agent_lines_added"`
   100→
   101→	// AgentLinesRemoved is total agent lines removed so far (base → last checkpoint).
   102→	// Always 0 for checkpoint 1 since there's no previous checkpoint to measure against.
   103→	AgentLinesRemoved int `json:"agent_lines_removed"`
   104→
   105→	// UserAddedPerFile tracks per-file user additions for accurate modification tracking.
   106→	// This enables distinguishing user self-modifications from agent modifications.
   107→	// See docs/architecture/attribution.md for details.
   108→	UserAddedPerFile map[string]int `json:"user_added_per_file,omitempty"`
   109→}
   110→
   111→// StateStore provides low-level operations for managing session state files.
   112→//
   113→// StateStore is a primitive for session state persistence. It is NOT the same as
   114→// the Sessions interface - it only handles state files in .git/entire-sessions/,
   115→// not the full session data which includes checkpoint content.
   116→//
   117→// Use StateStore directly in strategies for performance-critical state operations.
   118→// Use the Sessions interface (when implemented) for high-level session management.
   119→type StateStore struct {
   120→	// stateDir is the directory where session state files are stored
   121→	stateDir string
   122→}
   123→
   124→// NewStateStore creates a new state store.
   125→// Uses the git common dir to store session state (shared across worktrees).
   126→func NewStateStore() (*StateStore, error) {
   127→	commonDir, err := getGitCommonDir()
   128→	if err != nil {
   129→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
   130→	}
   131→	return &StateStore{
   132→		stateDir: filepath.Join(commonDir, sessionStateDirName),
   133→	}, nil
   134→}
   135→
   136→// NewStateStoreWithDir creates a new state store with a custom directory.
   137→// This is useful for testing.
   138→func NewStateStoreWithDir(stateDir string) *StateStore {
   139→	return &StateStore{stateDir: stateDir}
   140→}
   141→
   142→// Load loads the session state for the given session ID.
   143→// Returns (nil, nil) when session file doesn't exist (not an error condition).
   144→func (s *StateStore) Load(ctx context.Context, sessionID string) (*State, error) {
   145→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   146→
   147→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   148→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   149→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   150→	}
   151→
   152→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(sessionID)
   153→
   154→	data, err := os.ReadFile(stateFile) //nolint:gosec // stateFile is derived from sessionID
   155→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   156→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil,nil indicates session not found (expected case)
   157→	}
   158→	if err != nil {
   159→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state: %w", err)
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	var state State
   163→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   164→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal session state: %w", err)
   165→	}
   166→	return &state, nil
   167→}
   168→
   169→// Save saves the session state atomically.
   170→func (s *StateStore) Save(ctx context.Context, state *State) error {
   171→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   172→
   173→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   174→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(state.SessionID); err != nil {
   175→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   176→	}
   177→
   178→	if err := os.MkdirAll(s.stateDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   179→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session state directory: %w", err)
   180→	}
   181→
   182→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(state, "", "  ")
   183→	if err != nil {
   184→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal session state: %w", err)
   185→	}
   186→
   187→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(state.SessionID)
   188→
   189→	// Atomic write: write to temp file, then rename
   190→	tmpFile := stateFile + ".tmp"
   191→	if err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   192→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write session state: %w", err)
   193→	}
   194→	if err := os.Rename(tmpFile, stateFile); err != nil {
   195→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to rename session state file: %w", err)
   196→	}
   197→	return nil
   198→}
   199→
   200→// Clear removes the session state file for the given session ID.
   201→func (s *StateStore) Clear(ctx context.Context, sessionID string) error {
   202→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   203→
   204→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   205→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   206→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   207→	}
   208→
   209→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(sessionID)
   210→
   211→	if err := os.Remove(stateFile); err != nil {
   212→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   213→			return nil // Already gone, not an error
   214→		}
   215→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session state file: %w", err)
   216→	}
   217→	return nil
   218→}
   219→
   220→// RemoveAll removes the entire session state directory.
   221→// This is used during uninstall to completely remove all session state.
   222→func (s *StateStore) RemoveAll() error {
   223→	if err := os.RemoveAll(s.stateDir); err != nil {
   224→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session state directory: %w", err)
   225→	}
   226→	return nil
   227→}
   228→
   229→// List returns all session states.
   230→func (s *StateStore) List(ctx context.Context) ([]*State, error) {
   231→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   232→
   233→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(s.stateDir)
   234→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   235→		return nil, nil
   236→	}
   237→	if err != nil {
   238→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state directory: %w", err)
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	var states []*State
   242→	for _, entry := range entries {
   243→		if entry.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json") {
   244→			continue
   245→		}
   246→		if strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".tmp") {
   247→			continue // Skip temp files
   248→		}
   249→
   250→		sessionID := strings.TrimSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json")
   251→		state, err := s.Load(ctx, sessionID)
   252→		if err != nil {
   253→			continue // Skip corrupted state files
   254→		}
   255→		if state == nil {
   256→			continue
   257→		}
   258→
   259→		states = append(states, state)
   260→	}
   261→	return states, nil
   262→}
   263→
   264→// stateFilePath returns the path to a session state file.
   265→func (s *StateStore) stateFilePath(sessionID string) string {
   266→	return filepath.Join(s.stateDir, sessionID+".json")
   267→}
   268→
   269→// getGitCommonDir returns the path to the shared git directory.
   270→// In a regular checkout, this is .git/
   271→// In a worktree, this is the main repo's .git/ (not .git/worktrees/<name>/)
   272→func getGitCommonDir() (string, error) {
   273→	ctx := context.Background()
   274→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--git-common-dir")
   275→	cmd.Dir = "."
   276→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   277→	if err != nil {
   278→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
   279→	}
   280→
   281→	commonDir := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
   282→
   283→	// git rev-parse --git-common-dir returns relative paths from the working directory,
   284→	// so we need to make it absolute if it isn't already
   285→	if !filepath.IsAbs(commonDir) {
   286→		commonDir = filepath.Join(".", commonDir)
   287→	}
   288→
   289→	return filepath.Clean(commonDir), nil
   290→}

    18→// SessionState represents the state of an active session.
    19→type SessionState struct {
    20→	SessionID                string          `json:"session_id"`
    21→	BaseCommit               string          `json:"base_commit"`
    22→	WorktreePath             string          `json:"worktree_path,omitempty"` // Absolute path to the worktree root
    23→	WorktreeID               string          `json:"worktree_id,omitempty"`   // Internal git worktree identifier (empty for main worktree)
    24→	StartedAt                time.Time       `json:"started_at"`
    25→	EndedAt                  *time.Time      `json:"ended_at,omitempty"` // When the session was explicitly closed (nil = active or unclean exit)
    26→	CheckpointCount          int             `json:"checkpoint_count"`
    27→	CondensedTranscriptLines int             `json:"condensed_transcript_lines,omitempty"` // Lines already included in previous condensation

    29→	FilesTouched             []string        `json:"files_touched,omitempty"`              // Files modified/created/deleted during this session
    30→	LastCheckpointID         id.CheckpointID `json:"last_checkpoint_id,omitempty"`         // Checkpoint ID from last condensation, reused for subsequent commits without new content
    31→	AgentType                agent.AgentType `json:"agent_type,omitempty"`                 // Agent type identifier (e.g., "Claude Code", "Cursor")
    32→	TranscriptPath           string          `json:"transcript_path,omitempty"`            // Path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection)
    33→
    34→	// Token usage tracking (accumulated across all checkpoints in this session)
    35→	TokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage `json:"token_usage,omitempty"`
    36→
    37→	// Transcript position when session started (for multi-session checkpoints on entire/sessions)
    38→	TranscriptLinesAtStart      int    `json:"transcript_lines_at_start,omitempty"`
    39→	TranscriptIdentifierAtStart string `json:"transcript_identifier_at_start,omitempty"`
    40→
    41→	// PromptAttributions tracks user and agent line changes at each prompt start.
    42→	PromptAttributions []PromptAttribution `json:"prompt_attributions,omitempty"`
    43→
    44→	// PendingPromptAttribution holds attribution calculated at prompt start (before agent runs).
    45→	// This is moved to PromptAttributions when SaveChanges is called.
    46→	PendingPromptAttribution *PromptAttribution `json:"pending_prompt_attribution,omitempty"`
    47→}
    48→
    49→// PromptAttribution captures line-level attribution data at the start of each prompt,
    50→// calculated BEFORE the agent runs. This allows us to separate user edits (made between
    51→// prompts) from agent edits (made during prompt execution).
    52→//
    53→// Fields:
    54→//   - CheckpointNumber: Which checkpoint this attribution is for (1-indexed)
    55→//   - UserLinesAdded/Removed: User edits since the last checkpoint (lastCheckpoint → worktree)
    56→//   - AgentLinesAdded/Removed: Cumulative agent work so far (base → lastCheckpoint)
    57→//   - UserAddedPerFile: Per-file breakdown of user additions (for accurate modification tracking)
    58→//
    59→// Note: For checkpoint 1, AgentLinesAdded/Removed are always 0 because there is no previous
    60→// checkpoint to compare against. This doesn't mean the agent hasn't done work yet - it means
    61→// we can't measure cumulative agent work until after the first checkpoint is created.
    62→// These fields become meaningful starting from checkpoint 2.
    63→//
    64→// UserAddedPerFile enables accurate tracking of user self-modifications. When a user modifies
    65→// their own previously-added lines (not agent lines), we shouldn't subtract from the agent's
    66→// contribution. See docs/architecture/attribution.md for details.
    67→type PromptAttribution struct {
    68→	CheckpointNumber  int            `json:"checkpoint_number"`
    69→	UserLinesAdded    int            `json:"user_lines_added"`
    70→	UserLinesRemoved  int            `json:"user_lines_removed"`
    71→	AgentLinesAdded   int            `json:"agent_lines_added"`             // Always 0 for checkpoint 1 (no previous checkpoint)
    72→	AgentLinesRemoved int            `json:"agent_lines_removed"`           // Always 0 for checkpoint 1 (no previous checkpoint)
    73→	UserAddedPerFile  map[string]int `json:"user_added_per_file,omitempty"` // Per-file user additions for modification tracking
    74→}
    75→
    76→// CheckpointInfo represents checkpoint metadata stored on the sessions branch.
    77→// Metadata is stored at sharded path: <checkpoint_id[:2]>/<checkpoint_id[2:]>/
    78→type CheckpointInfo struct {
    79→	CheckpointID     id.CheckpointID `json:"checkpoint_id"` // 12-hex-char from Entire-Checkpoint trailer, used as directory path
    80→	SessionID        string          `json:"session_id"`
    81→	CreatedAt        time.Time       `json:"created_at"`
    82→	CheckpointsCount int             `json:"checkpoints_count"`
    83→	FilesTouched     []string        `json:"files_touched"`
    84→	Agent            agent.AgentType `json:"agent,omitempty"` // Human-readable agent name (e.g., "Claude Code")
    85→	IsTask           bool            `json:"is_task,omitempty"`
    86→	ToolUseID        string          `json:"tool_use_id,omitempty"`
    87→	SessionCount     int             `json:"session_count,omitempty"` // Number of sessions (1 if omitted)
    88→	SessionIDs       []string        `json:"session_ids,omitempty"`   // All session IDs in this checkpoint
    89→}
    90→
    91→// CondenseResult contains the result of a session condensation operation.
    92→type CondenseResult struct {
    93→	CheckpointID         id.CheckpointID // 12-hex-char from Entire-Checkpoint trailer, used as directory path
    94→	SessionID            string
    95→	CheckpointsCount     int
    96→	FilesTouched         []string
    97→	TotalTranscriptLines int // Total lines in transcript after this condensation
    98→}

     1→// Package strategy provides an interface for different git strategies
     2→// that can be used to save and manage Claude Code session changes.
     3→package strategy
     4→

     6→	"encoding/json"

    15→
    16→// ErrNoMetadata is returned when a commit does not have an Entire metadata trailer.
    17→var ErrNoMetadata = errors.New("commit has no entire metadata")
    18→
    19→// ErrNoSession is returned when no session info is available.
    20→var ErrNoSession = errors.New("no session info available")
    21→
    22→// ErrNotTaskCheckpoint is returned when a rewind point is not a task checkpoint.
    23→var ErrNotTaskCheckpoint = errors.New("not a task checkpoint")
    24→
    25→// ErrNotImplemented is returned when a feature is not yet implemented.
    26→var ErrNotImplemented = errors.New("not implemented")
    27→
    28→// SessionIDConflictError is returned when trying to start a new session
    29→// but the shadow branch already has commits from a different session ID.
    30→// This prevents orphaning existing session work.
    31→type SessionIDConflictError struct {
    32→	ExistingSession string // Session ID found in the shadow branch
    33→	NewSession      string // Session ID being initialized
    34→	ShadowBranch    string // The shadow branch name (e.g., "entire/abc1234")
    35→}
    36→
    37→func (e *SessionIDConflictError) Error() string {
    38→	return "session ID conflict: shadow branch has commits from a different session"
    39→}
    40→
    41→// ExtractToolUseIDFromTaskMetadataDir extracts the ToolUseID from a task metadata directory path.
    42→// Task metadata dirs have format: .entire/metadata/<session>/tasks/<toolUseID>
    43→// Returns empty string if not a task metadata directory.
    44→func ExtractToolUseIDFromTaskMetadataDir(metadataDir string) string {
    45→	parts := strings.Split(metadataDir, "/")
    46→	if len(parts) >= 2 && parts[len(parts)-2] == "tasks" {
    47→		return parts[len(parts)-1]
    48→	}
    49→	return ""
    50→}
    51→
    52→// SessionInfo contains information about the current session state.
    53→// This is used to generate trailers for linking commits to their AI session.
    54→type SessionInfo struct {
    55→	// SessionID is the session identifier extracted from the latest commit's metadata
    56→	SessionID string
    57→
    58→	// Reference is a strategy-specific reference string.
    59→	// For manual-commit strategy: "entire/abc1234" (the shadow branch name)
    60→	// Empty for commit strategy (metadata is in the same commit).
    61→	Reference string
    62→
    63→	// CommitHash is the full SHA of the commit containing the session metadata.
    64→	// Empty for commit strategy.
    65→	CommitHash string
    66→}

    95→	// CheckpointID is the stable 12-hex-char identifier for logs-only points.

   100→	// Agent is the human-readable name of the agent that created this checkpoint

   284→// TaskMetadataDir returns the path to a task's metadata directory
   285→// within the session metadata directory.
   286→func TaskMetadataDir(sessionMetadataDir, toolUseID string) string {
   287→	return sessionMetadataDir + "/tasks/" + toolUseID
   288→}
   289→
   290→// ReadTaskCheckpoint reads the checkpoint.json file from a task metadata directory.
   291→// This is used during rewind to get the checkpoint UUID for transcript truncation.
   292→func ReadTaskCheckpoint(taskMetadataDir string) (*TaskCheckpoint, error) {
   293→	checkpointFile := taskMetadataDir + "/checkpoint.json"
   294→
   295→	data, err := os.ReadFile(checkpointFile) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled git metadata path
   296→	if err != nil {
   297→		return nil, err
   298→	}
   299→
   300→	var checkpoint TaskCheckpoint
   301→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &checkpoint); err != nil {
   302→		return nil, err
   303→	}
   304→
   305→	return &checkpoint, nil
   306→}
   307→
   308→// Strategy defines the interface for git operation strategies.
   309→// Different implementations can use commits, branches, stashes, etc.
   310→//
   311→// Note: State capture (tracking untracked files before a session) is handled
   312→// by the CLI layer, not the strategy. The strategy receives pre-computed
   313→// file lists in SaveContext.
   314→type Strategy interface {
   315→	// Name returns the strategy identifier (e.g., "commit", "branch", "stash")
   316→	Name() string
   317→
   318→	// Description returns a human-readable description for the setup wizard
   319→	Description() string
   320→
   321→	// AllowsMainBranch returns true if this strategy can safely operate on the
   322→	// default branch (main/master). Strategies that don't modify the working
   323→	// branch's commit history (like manual-commit strategies) can return true.
   324→	// Strategies that create commits on the working branch should return false
   325→	// to prevent polluting main branch history.
   326→	AllowsMainBranch() bool
   327→
   328→	// ValidateRepository checks if the repository is in a valid state
   329→	// for this strategy to operate. Returns an error if validation fails.
   330→	ValidateRepository() error
   331→
   332→	// SaveChanges is called on Stop to save all session changes
   333→	// using this strategy's approach (commit, branch, stash, etc.)
   334→	SaveChanges(ctx SaveContext) error
   335→
   336→	// SaveTaskCheckpoint is called by PostToolUse[Task] hook when a subagent completes.
   337→	// Creates a checkpoint commit with task metadata for later rewind.
   338→	// Different strategies may handle this differently:
   339→	// - Commit strategy: commits to active branch
   340→	// - Manual-commit strategy: commits to shadow branch
   341→	// - Auto-commit strategy: commits logs to shadow only (code deferred to Stop)
   342→	SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx TaskCheckpointContext) error
   343→
   344→	// GetRewindPoints returns available points to rewind to.
   345→	// The limit parameter controls the maximum number of points to return.
   346→	GetRewindPoints(limit int) ([]RewindPoint, error)
   347→
   348→	// Rewind restores the repository to the given rewind point.
   349→	// The metadataDir in the point is used to restore the session transcript.
   350→	Rewind(point RewindPoint) error
   351→
   352→	// CanRewind checks if rewinding is currently possible.
   353→	// Returns (canRewind, reason if not, error)
   354→	CanRewind() (bool, string, error)
   355→
   356→	// PreviewRewind returns what will happen if rewinding to the given point.
   357→	// This allows showing warnings about files that will be deleted before the rewind.
   358→	// Returns nil if preview is not supported (e.g., auto-commit strategy).
   359→	PreviewRewind(point RewindPoint) (*RewindPreview, error)
   360→
   361→	// GetTaskCheckpoint returns the task checkpoint for a given rewind point.
   362→	// For strategies that store checkpoints in git (auto-commit), this reads from the branch.
   363→	// For strategies that store checkpoints on disk (commit, manual-commit), this reads from the filesystem.
   364→	// Returns nil, nil if not a task checkpoint or checkpoint not found.
   365→	GetTaskCheckpoint(point RewindPoint) (*TaskCheckpoint, error)
   366→
   367→	// GetTaskCheckpointTranscript returns the session transcript for a task checkpoint.
   368→	// For strategies that store transcripts in git (auto-commit), this reads from the branch.
   369→	// For strategies that store transcripts on disk (commit, manual-commit), this reads from the filesystem.
   370→	GetTaskCheckpointTranscript(point RewindPoint) ([]byte, error)
   371→
   372→	// GetSessionInfo returns session information for linking commits.
   373→	// This is used by the context command to generate trailers.
   374→	// Returns ErrNoSession if no session info is available.
   375→	GetSessionInfo() (*SessionInfo, error)
   376→
   377→	// EnsureSetup ensures the strategy's required setup is in place,
   378→	// installing any missing pieces (git hooks, gitignore entries, etc.).
   379→	// Returns nil if setup is complete or was successfully installed.
   380→	EnsureSetup() error
   381→
   382→	// NOTE: ListSessions and GetSession are standalone functions in session.go.
   383→	// They read from entire/sessions and merge with SessionSource if implemented.
   384→
   385→	// GetMetadataRef returns a reference to the metadata commit for the given checkpoint.
   386→	// Format: "<branch>@<commit-sha>" (e.g., "entire/sessions@abc123").
   387→	// Returns empty string if not applicable (e.g., commit strategy with filesystem metadata).
   388→	GetMetadataRef(checkpoint Checkpoint) string
   389→
   390→	// GetSessionMetadataRef returns a reference to the most recent metadata commit for a session.
   391→	// Format: "<branch>@<commit-sha>" (e.g., "entire/sessions@abc123").
   392→	// Returns empty string if not applicable or session not found.
   393→	GetSessionMetadataRef(sessionID string) string
   394→
   395→	// GetSessionContext returns the context.md content for a session.
   396→	// Returns empty string if not available.
   397→	GetSessionContext(sessionID string) string
   398→
   399→	// GetCheckpointLog returns the session transcript for a specific checkpoint.
   400→	// For strategies that store transcripts in git branches (auto-commit, manual-commit),
   401→	// this reads from the checkpoint's commit tree.
   402→	// For strategies that store on disk (commit), reads from the filesystem.
   403→	// Returns ErrNoMetadata if transcript is not available.
   404→	GetCheckpointLog(checkpoint Checkpoint) ([]byte, error)
   405→}
   406→
   407→// SessionInitializer is an optional interface for strategies that need to
   408→// initialize session state when a user prompt is submitted.
   409→// Strategies like manual-commit use this to create session state files that
   410→// the git prepare-commit-msg hook can detect.
   411→type SessionInitializer interface {
   412→	// InitializeSession creates session state for a new session.
   413→	// Called during UserPromptSubmit hook before any checkpoints are created.
   414→	// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
   415→	// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
   416→	InitializeSession(sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string) error
   417→}
   418→
   419→// PrepareCommitMsgHandler is an optional interface for strategies that need to
   420→// handle the git prepare-commit-msg hook.
   421→type PrepareCommitMsgHandler interface {
   422→	// PrepareCommitMsg is called by the git prepare-commit-msg hook.
   423→	// It can modify the commit message file to add trailers, etc.
   424→	// The source parameter indicates how the commit was initiated:
   425→	//   - "" or "template": normal editor flow
   426→	//   - "message": using -m or -F flag
   427→	//   - "merge": merge commit
   428→	//   - "squash": squash merge
   429→	//   - "commit": amend with -c/-C
   430→	// Should return nil on errors to not block commits (log warnings to stderr).
   431→	PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile string, source string) error
   432→}
   433→
   434→// PostCommitHandler is an optional interface for strategies that need to
   435→// handle the git post-commit hook.
   436→type PostCommitHandler interface {
   437→	// PostCommit is called by the git post-commit hook after a commit is created.
   438→	// Used to perform actions like condensing session data after commits.
   439→	// Should return nil on errors to not block subsequent operations (log warnings to stderr).
   440→	PostCommit() error
   441→}
   442→
   443→// CommitMsgHandler is an optional interface for strategies that need to
   444→// handle the git commit-msg hook.
   445→type CommitMsgHandler interface {
   446→	// CommitMsg is called by the git commit-msg hook after the user edits the message.
   447→	// Used to validate or modify the final commit message before the commit is created.
   448→	// If this returns an error, the commit is aborted.
   449→	CommitMsg(commitMsgFile string) error
   450→}
   451→
   452→// PrePushHandler is an optional interface for strategies that need to
   453→// handle the git pre-push hook.
   454→type PrePushHandler interface {
   455→	// PrePush is called by the git pre-push hook before pushing to a remote.
   456→	// Used to push session branches (e.g., entire/sessions) alongside user pushes.
   457→	// The remote parameter is the name of the remote being pushed to.
   458→	// Should return nil on errors to not block pushes (log warnings to stderr).
   459→	PrePush(remote string) error
   460→}
   461→
   462→// LogsOnlyRestorer is an optional interface for strategies that support
   463→// restoring session logs without file state restoration.
   464→// This is used for "logs-only" rewind points where only the session transcript
   465→// can be restored (file state requires git checkout).
   466→type LogsOnlyRestorer interface {
   467→	// RestoreLogsOnly restores session logs from a logs-only rewind point.
   468→	// Does not modify the working directory - only restores the transcript
   469→	// to Claude's project directory.
   470→	// If force is false, prompts for confirmation when local logs have newer timestamps.
   471→	RestoreLogsOnly(point RewindPoint, force bool) error
   472→}
   473→
   474→// SessionResetter is an optional interface for strategies that support
   475→// resetting session state and shadow branches.
   476→// This is used by the "rewind reset" command to clean up shadow branches
   477→// and session state when a user wants to start fresh.
   478→type SessionResetter interface {
   479→	// Reset deletes the shadow branch and session state for the current HEAD.
   480→	// Returns nil if there's nothing to reset (no shadow branch).
   481→	Reset() error
   482→}
   483→
   484→// ConcurrentSessionChecker is an optional interface for strategies that support
   485→// detecting and warning about concurrent sessions with uncommitted changes.
   486→// This is used by the UserPromptSubmit hook to block the first prompt when
   487→// another session has uncommitted checkpoints on the same base commit.
   488→type ConcurrentSessionChecker interface {
   489→	// HasOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints returns a session state if there's another
   490→	// active session with uncommitted checkpoints on the same base commit.
   491→	// Returns nil, nil if no such session exists.
   492→	HasOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(currentSessionID string) (*SessionState, error)
   493→}
   494→
   495→// SessionSource is an optional interface for strategies that provide additional
   496→// sessions beyond those stored on the entire/sessions branch.
   497→// For example, manual-commit strategy provides active sessions from .git/entire-sessions/
   498→// that haven't yet been condensed to entire/sessions.
   499→//
   500→// ListSessions() automatically discovers all registered strategies, checks if they
   501→// implement SessionSource, and merges their additional sessions by ID.
   502→type SessionSource interface {
   503→	// GetAdditionalSessions returns sessions not yet on entire/sessions branch.
   504→	GetAdditionalSessions() ([]*Session, error)
   505→}
   506→
   507→// OrphanedItemsLister is an optional interface for strategies that can identify
   508→// orphaned items (shadow branches, session states, checkpoints) that should be
   509→// cleaned up. This is used by the "entire session cleanup" command.
   510→//
   511→// ListAllCleanupItems() automatically discovers all registered strategies, checks
   512→// if they implement OrphanedItemsLister, and combines their orphaned items.
   513→type OrphanedItemsLister interface {
   514→	// ListOrphanedItems returns items created by this strategy that are now orphaned.
   515→	// Each strategy defines what "orphaned" means for its own data structures.
   516→	ListOrphanedItems() ([]CleanupItem, error)
   517→}
