    27→// askConfirmTTY prompts the user for a yes/no confirmation via /dev/tty.
    28→// This works even when stdin is redirected (e.g., git commit -m).
    29→// Returns true for yes, false for no. If TTY is unavailable, returns the default.
    30→// If context is non-empty, it is displayed on a separate line before the prompt.
    31→func askConfirmTTY(prompt string, context string, defaultYes bool) bool {
    32→	// Open /dev/tty for both reading and writing
    33→	// This is the controlling terminal, which works even when stdin/stderr are redirected
    34→	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
    35→	if err != nil {
    36→		// Can't open TTY (e.g., running in CI), use default
    37→		return defaultYes
    38→	}
    39→	defer tty.Close()
    40→
    41→	// Show context if provided
    42→	if context != "" {
    43→		fmt.Fprintf(tty, "%s\n", context)
    44→	}
    45→
    46→	// Show prompt with default indicator
    47→	// Write to tty directly, not stderr, since git hooks may redirect stderr to /dev/null
    48→	var hint string
    49→	if defaultYes {
    50→		hint = "[Y/n]"
    51→	} else {
    52→		hint = "[y/N]"
    53→	}
    54→	fmt.Fprintf(tty, "%s %s ", prompt, hint)
    55→
    56→	// Read response
    57→	reader := bufio.NewReader(tty)
    58→	response, err := reader.ReadString('\n')
    59→	if err != nil {
    60→		return defaultYes
    61→	}
    62→
    63→	response = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(response))
    64→	switch response {
    65→	case "y", "yes":
    66→		return true
    67→	case "n", "no":
    68→		return false
    69→	default:
    70→		// Empty or invalid input - use default
    71→		return defaultYes
    72→	}
    73→}
    74→
    75→// CommitMsg is called by the git commit-msg hook after the user edits the message.
    76→// If the message contains only our trailer (no actual user content), strip it
    77→// so git will abort the commit due to empty message.
    78→//
    79→//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
    80→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CommitMsg(commitMsgFile string) error {
    81→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from git hook
    82→	if err != nil {
    83→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
    84→	}
    85→
    86→	message := string(content)
    87→
    88→	// Check if our trailer is present (ParseCheckpoint validates format, so found==true means valid)
    89→	if _, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); !found {
    90→		// No trailer, nothing to do
    91→		return nil
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	// Check if there's any user content (non-comment, non-trailer lines)
    95→	if !hasUserContent(message) {
    96→		// No user content - strip the trailer so git aborts
    97→		message = stripCheckpointTrailer(message)
    98→		if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); err != nil {
    99→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   100→		}
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	return nil
   104→}
   105→
   106→// hasUserContent checks if the message has any content besides comments and our trailer.
   107→func hasUserContent(message string) bool {
   108→	trailerPrefix := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ":"
   109→	for _, line := range strings.Split(message, "\n") {
   110→		trimmed := strings.TrimSpace(line)
   111→		// Skip empty lines
   112→		if trimmed == "" {
   113→			continue
   114→		}
   115→		// Skip comment lines
   116→		if strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, "#") {
   117→			continue
   118→		}
   119→		// Skip our trailer line
   120→		if strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, trailerPrefix) {
   121→			continue
   122→		}
   123→		// Found user content
   124→		return true
   125→	}
   126→	return false
   127→}
   128→
   129→// stripCheckpointTrailer removes the Entire-Checkpoint trailer line from the message.
   130→func stripCheckpointTrailer(message string) string {
   131→	trailerPrefix := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ":"
   132→	var result []string
   133→	for _, line := range strings.Split(message, "\n") {
   134→		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.TrimSpace(line), trailerPrefix) {
   135→			result = append(result, line)
   136→		}
   137→	}
   138→	return strings.Join(result, "\n")
   139→}
   140→
   141→// isGitSequenceOperation checks if git is currently in the middle of a rebase,
   142→// cherry-pick, or revert operation. During these operations, commits are being
   143→// replayed and should not be linked to agent sessions.
   144→//
   145→// Detects:
   146→//   - rebase: .git/rebase-merge/ or .git/rebase-apply/ directories
   147→//   - cherry-pick: .git/CHERRY_PICK_HEAD file
   148→//   - revert: .git/REVERT_HEAD file
   149→func isGitSequenceOperation() bool {
   150→	// Get git directory (handles worktrees and relative paths correctly)
   151→	gitDir, err := GetGitDir()
   152→	if err != nil {
   153→		return false // Can't determine, assume not in sequence operation
   154→	}
   155→
   156→	// Check for rebase state directories
   157→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "rebase-merge")); err == nil {
   158→		return true
   159→	}
   160→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "rebase-apply")); err == nil {
   161→		return true
   162→	}
   163→
   164→	// Check for cherry-pick and revert state files
   165→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "CHERRY_PICK_HEAD")); err == nil {
   166→		return true
   167→	}
   168→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "REVERT_HEAD")); err == nil {
   169→		return true
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	return false
   173→}
   174→
   175→// PrepareCommitMsg is called by the git prepare-commit-msg hook.
   176→// Adds an Entire-Checkpoint trailer to the commit message with a stable checkpoint ID.
   177→// Only adds a trailer if there's actually new session content to condense.
   178→// The actual condensation happens in PostCommit - if the user removes the trailer,
   179→// the commit will not be linked to the session (useful for "manual" commits).
   180→// For amended commits, preserves the existing checkpoint ID.
   181→//
   182→// The source parameter indicates how the commit was initiated:
   183→//   - "" or "template": normal editor flow - adds trailer with explanatory comment
   184→//   - "message": using -m or -F flag - prompts user interactively via /dev/tty
   185→//   - "merge", "squash", "commit": skip trailer entirely (auto-generated or amend commits)
   186→//
   187→
   188→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile string, source string) error {
   189→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   190→
   191→	// Skip during rebase, cherry-pick, or revert operations
   192→	// These are replaying existing commits and should not be linked to agent sessions
   193→	if isGitSequenceOperation() {
   194→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: skipped during git sequence operation",
   195→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   196→			slog.String("source", source),
   197→		)
   198→		return nil
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	// Skip for merge, squash, and commit (amend) sources
   202→	// These are auto-generated or reusing existing messages - not from Claude sessions
   203→	switch source {
   204→	case "merge", "squash", "commit":
   205→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: skipped for source",
   206→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   207→			slog.String("source", source),
   208→		)
   209→		return nil
   210→	}
   211→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   212→	if err != nil {
   213→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   217→	if err != nil {
   218→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   219→	}
   220→
   221→	// Find all active sessions for this worktree
   222→	// We match by worktree (not BaseCommit) because the user may have made
   223→	// intermediate commits without entering new prompts, causing HEAD to diverge
   224→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   225→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   226→		// No active sessions or error listing - silently skip (hooks must be resilient)
   227→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: no active sessions",
   228→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   229→			slog.String("source", source),
   230→		)
   231→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
   232→	}
   233→
   234→	// Check if any session has new content to condense
   235→	sessionsWithContent := s.filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo, sessions)
   236→
   237→	// Determine which checkpoint ID to use
   238→	var checkpointID id.CheckpointID
   239→	var hasNewContent bool
   240→	var reusedSession *SessionState
   241→
   242→	if len(sessionsWithContent) > 0 {
   243→		// New content exists - will generate new checkpoint ID below
   244→		hasNewContent = true
   245→	} else {
   246→		// No new content - check if any session has a LastCheckpointID to reuse
   247→		// This handles the case where user splits Claude's work across multiple commits
   248→		// Reuse if: LastCheckpointID exists AND (FilesTouched is empty OR files overlap)
   249→		// - FilesTouched empty: commits made before session stop, reuse checkpoint
   250→		// - FilesTouched populated: only reuse if files overlap (prevents unrelated commits from reusing)
   251→
   252→		// Get current HEAD to filter sessions
   253→		head, err := repo.Head()
   254→		if err != nil {
   255→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   256→		}
   257→		currentHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
   258→
   259→		// Filter to sessions where BaseCommit matches current HEAD
   260→		// This prevents reusing checkpoint IDs from old sessions
   261→		// Note: BaseCommit is kept current both when new content is condensed (in the
   262→		// condensation process) and when no new content is found (via PostCommit when
   263→		// reusing checkpoint IDs). If none match, we don't add a trailer rather than
   264→		// falling back to old sessions which could have stale checkpoint IDs.
   265→		var currentSessions []*SessionState
   266→		for _, session := range sessions {
   267→			if session.BaseCommit == currentHeadHash {
   268→				currentSessions = append(currentSessions, session)
   269→			}
   270→		}
   271→
   272→		if len(currentSessions) == 0 {
   273→			// No sessions match current HEAD - don't try to reuse checkpoint IDs
   274→			// from old sessions as they may be stale
   275→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: no sessions match current HEAD",
   276→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   277→				slog.String("source", source),
   278→				slog.String("current_head", currentHeadHash[:7]),
   279→				slog.Int("total_sessions", len(sessions)),
   280→			)
   281→			return nil
   282→		}
   283→
   284→		stagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo)
   285→		for _, session := range currentSessions {
   286→			if !session.LastCheckpointID.IsEmpty() &&
   287→				(len(session.FilesTouched) == 0 || hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, session.FilesTouched)) {
   288→				checkpointID = session.LastCheckpointID
   289→				reusedSession = session
   290→				break
   291→			}
   292→		}
   293→		if checkpointID.IsEmpty() {
   294→			// No new content and no previous checkpoint to reference (or staged files are unrelated)
   295→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: no content to link",
   296→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   297→				slog.String("source", source),
   298→				slog.Int("sessions_found", len(sessions)),
   299→				slog.Int("sessions_with_content", len(sessionsWithContent)),
   300→			)
   301→			return nil
   302→		}
   303→	}
   304→
   305→	// Read current commit message
   306→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
   307→	if err != nil {
   308→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   309→	}
   310→
   311→	message := string(content)
   312→
   313→	// Get or generate checkpoint ID (ParseCheckpoint validates format, so found==true means valid)
   314→	if existingCpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {
   315→		// Trailer already exists (e.g., amend) - keep it
   316→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: trailer already exists",
   317→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   318→			slog.String("source", source),
   319→			slog.String("existing_checkpoint_id", existingCpID.String()),
   320→		)
   321→		return nil
   322→	}
   323→
   324→	if hasNewContent {
   325→		// New content: generate new checkpoint ID
   326→		cpID, err := id.Generate()
   327→		if err != nil {
   328→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   329→		}
   330→		checkpointID = cpID
   331→	}
   332→	// Otherwise checkpointID is already set to LastCheckpointID from above
   333→
   334→	// Determine agent type and last prompt from session
   335→	agentType := DefaultAgentType // default for backward compatibility
   336→	var lastPrompt string
   337→	if hasNewContent && len(sessionsWithContent) > 0 {
   338→		session := sessionsWithContent[0]
   339→		if session.AgentType != "" {
   340→			agentType = session.AgentType
   341→		}
   342→		lastPrompt = s.getLastPrompt(repo, session)
   343→	} else if reusedSession != nil {
   344→		// Reusing checkpoint from existing session - get agent type and prompt from that session
   345→		if reusedSession.AgentType != "" {
   346→			agentType = reusedSession.AgentType
   347→		}
   348→		lastPrompt = s.getLastPrompt(repo, reusedSession)
   349→	}
   350→
   351→	// Prepare prompt for display: collapse newlines/whitespace, then truncate (rune-safe)
   352→	displayPrompt := stringutil.TruncateRunes(stringutil.CollapseWhitespace(lastPrompt), 80, "...")
   353→
   354→	// Add trailer differently based on commit source
   355→	if source == "message" {
   356→		// Using -m or -F: ask user interactively whether to add trailer
   357→		// (comments won't be stripped by git in this mode)
   358→
   359→		// Build context string for interactive prompt
   360→		var promptContext string
   361→		if displayPrompt != "" {
   362→			promptContext = "You have an active " + string(agentType) + " session.\nLast Prompt: " + displayPrompt
   363→		}
   364→
   365→		if !askConfirmTTY("Link this commit to "+string(agentType)+" session context?", promptContext, true) {
   366→			// User declined - don't add trailer
   367→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: user declined trailer",
   368→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   369→				slog.String("source", source),
   370→			)
   371→			return nil
   372→		}
   373→		message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, checkpointID)
   374→	} else {
   375→		// Normal editor flow: add trailer with explanatory comment (will be stripped by git)
   376→		message = addCheckpointTrailerWithComment(message, checkpointID, string(agentType), displayPrompt)
   377→	}
   378→
   379→	logging.Info(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: trailer added",
   380→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   381→		slog.String("source", source),
   382→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   383→		slog.Bool("has_new_content", hasNewContent),
   384→	)
   385→
   386→	// Write updated message back
   387→	if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); err != nil {
   388→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   389→	}
   390→
   391→	return nil
   392→}
   393→
   394→// PostCommit is called by the git post-commit hook after a commit is created.
   395→// Checks if the commit has an Entire-Checkpoint trailer and if so, condenses
   396→// session data from shadow branches to entire/sessions.
   397→// If the user removed the trailer during commit message editing, this is treated
   398→// as a "manual" commit and no condensation happens.
   399→//
   400→//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
   401→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PostCommit() error {
   402→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   403→
   404→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   405→	if err != nil {
   406→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   407→	}
   408→
   409→	// Get HEAD commit to check for trailer
   410→	head, err := repo.Head()
   411→	if err != nil {
   412→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   413→	}
   414→
   415→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   416→	if err != nil {
   417→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   418→	}
   419→
   420→	// Check if commit has checkpoint trailer (ParseCheckpoint validates format)
   421→	checkpointID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(commit.Message)
   422→	if !found {
   423→		// No trailer - user removed it, treat as manual commit
   424→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: no checkpoint trailer",
   425→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   426→		)
   427→		return nil
   428→	}
   429→
   430→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   431→	if err != nil {
   432→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   433→	}
   434→
   435→	// Find all active sessions for this worktree
   436→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   437→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   438→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: no active sessions despite trailer",
   439→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   440→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   441→		)
   442→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
   443→	}
   444→
   445→	// Filter to sessions with new content
   446→	sessionsWithContent := s.filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo, sessions)
   447→	if len(sessionsWithContent) == 0 {
   448→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: no new content to condense",
   449→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   450→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   451→			slog.Int("sessions_found", len(sessions)),
   452→		)
   453→		// Still update BaseCommit for all sessions in this worktree
   454→		// This prevents stale BaseCommit when commits happen without condensation
   455→		// (e.g., when reusing a previous checkpoint ID for split commits)
   456→		newHead := head.Hash().String()
   457→		for _, state := range sessions {
   458→			if state.BaseCommit != newHead {
   459→				state.BaseCommit = newHead
   460→				if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   461→					logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: failed to update session BaseCommit",
   462→						slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   463→						slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   464→					)
   465→				}
   466→			}
   467→		}
   468→		return nil
   469→	}
   470→
   471→	// Track shadow branch names to clean up after successful condensation
   472→	shadowBranchesToDelete := make(map[string]struct{})
   473→
   474→	// Condense sessions that have new content
   475→	for _, state := range sessionsWithContent {
   476→		// Compute shadow branch name BEFORE condensation modifies state.BaseCommit
   477→		shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   478→
   479→		result, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state)
   480→		if err != nil {
   481→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: condensation failed for session %s: %v\n",
   482→				state.SessionID, err)
   483→			continue
   484→		}
   485→
   486→		// Track this shadow branch for cleanup
   487→		shadowBranchesToDelete[shadowBranchName] = struct{}{}
   488→
   489→		// Update session state for the new base commit
   490→		// After condensation, the session continues from the NEW commit (HEAD), so we:
   491→		// 1. Update BaseCommit to new HEAD - session now tracks from new commit
   492→		// 2. Reset CheckpointCount to 0 - no checkpoints exist on new shadow branch yet
   493→		// 3. Update CondensedTranscriptLines - track transcript position for detecting new content
   494→		// 4. Clear PromptAttributions - they were already used in condensation, reset for next cycle
   495→		//
   496→		// This is critical: if we don't update BaseCommit, listAllSessionStates will try
   497→		// to find shadow branch for old commit (which gets deleted), and since CheckpointCount > 0,
   498→		// it will clean up (delete) the session state file. By updating to new HEAD with
   499→		// CheckpointCount = 0, the session is preserved even without a shadow branch.
   500→		state.BaseCommit = head.Hash().String()
   501→		state.CheckpointCount = 0
   502→		state.CondensedTranscriptLines = result.TotalTranscriptLines
   503→
   504→		// Clear attribution tracking - condensation already used these values
   505→		// If we don't clear them, they'll be double-counted in the next condensation
   506→		state.PromptAttributions = nil
   507→		state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil
   508→
   509→		// Save checkpoint ID so subsequent commits without new content can reuse it
   510→		state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID
   511→
   512→		if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   513→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", err)
   514→		}
   515→
   516→		shortID := state.SessionID
   517→		if len(shortID) > 8 {
   518→			shortID = shortID[:8]
   519→		}
   520→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Condensed session %s: %s (%d checkpoints)\n",
   521→			shortID, result.CheckpointID, result.CheckpointsCount)
   522→
   523→		// Log condensation
   524→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   525→		logging.Info(logCtx, "session condensed",
   526→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   527→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", result.CheckpointID.String()),
   528→			slog.Int("checkpoints_condensed", result.CheckpointsCount),
   529→			slog.Int("transcript_lines", result.TotalTranscriptLines),
   530→		)
   531→	}
   532→
   533→	// Clean up shadow branches after successful condensation
   534→	// Data is now preserved on entire/sessions, so shadow branches are no longer needed
   535→	for shadowBranchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {
   536→		if err := deleteShadowBranch(repo, shadowBranchName); err != nil {
   537→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to clean up %s: %v\n", shadowBranchName, err)
   538→		} else {
   539→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Cleaned up shadow branch: %s\n", shadowBranchName)
   540→
   541→			// Log branch cleanup
   542→			logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   543→			logging.Info(logCtx, "shadow branch deleted",
   544→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   545→				slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   546→			)
   547→		}
   548→	}
   549→
   550→	return nil
   551→}
   552→
   553→// filterSessionsWithNewContent returns sessions that have new transcript content
   554→// beyond what was already condensed.
   555→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo *git.Repository, sessions []*SessionState) []*SessionState {
   556→	var result []*SessionState
   557→
   558→	for _, state := range sessions {
   559→		hasNew, err := s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
   560→		if err != nil {
   561→			// On error, include the session (fail open for hooks)
   562→			result = append(result, state)
   563→			continue
   564→		}
   565→		if hasNew {
   566→			result = append(result, state)
   567→		}
   568→	}
   569→
   570→	return result
   571→}
   572→
   573→// sessionHasNewContent checks if a session has new transcript content
   574→// beyond what was already condensed.
   575→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContent(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
   576→	// Get shadow branch
   577→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   578→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   579→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   580→	if err != nil {
   581→		// No shadow branch means no Stop has happened since the last condensation.
   582→		// However, the agent may have done work (including commits) without a Stop.
   583→		// Check the live transcript to detect this scenario.
   584→		return s.sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo, state)
   585→	}
   586→
   587→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   588→	if err != nil {
   589→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   590→	}
   591→
   592→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   593→	if err != nil {
   594→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   595→	}
   596→
   597→	// Look for transcript file
   598→	metadataDir := paths.EntireMetadataDir + "/" + state.SessionID
   599→	var transcriptLines int
   600→
   601→	if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   602→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   603→			transcriptLines = countTranscriptLines(content)
   604→		}
   605→	} else if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); fileErr == nil {
   606→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   607→			transcriptLines = countTranscriptLines(content)
   608→		}
   609→	}
   610→
   611→	// Has new content if there are more lines than already condensed
   612→	return transcriptLines > state.CondensedTranscriptLines, nil
   613→}
   614→
   615→// countTranscriptLines counts lines in a transcript, matching the counting method used
   616→// in extractSessionData for consistency. This trims trailing empty lines (from final \n
   617→// in JSONL) but includes empty lines in the middle of the file.
   618→func countTranscriptLines(content string) int {
   619→	lines := strings.Split(content, "\n")
   620→	// Trim trailing empty lines (from final \n in JSONL)
   621→	for len(lines) > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(lines[len(lines)-1]) == "" {
   622→		lines = lines[:len(lines)-1]
   623→	}
   624→	return len(lines)
   625→}
   626→
   627→// sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript checks if a session has new content
   628→// by examining the live transcript file. This is used when no shadow branch exists
   629→// (i.e., no Stop has happened yet) but the agent may have done work.
   630→//
   631→// Returns true if:
   632→//  1. The transcript has grown since the last condensation, AND
   633→//  2. The new transcript portion contains file modifications, AND
   634→//  3. At least one modified file overlaps with the currently staged files
   635→//
   636→// The overlap check ensures we don't add checkpoint trailers to commits that are
   637→// unrelated to the agent's recent changes.
   638→//
   639→// This handles the scenario where the agent commits mid-session before Stop.
   640→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
   641→	// Need both transcript path and agent type to analyze
   642→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" || state.AgentType == "" {
   643→		return false, nil
   644→	}
   645→
   646→	// Get the agent for transcript analysis
   647→	ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(state.AgentType)
   648→	if err != nil {
   649→		return false, nil //nolint:nilerr // Unknown agent type, fail gracefully
   650→	}
   651→
   652→	// Cast to TranscriptAnalyzer
   653→	analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer)
   654→	if !ok {
   655→		return false, nil // Agent doesn't support transcript analysis
   656→	}
   657→
   658→	// Get current transcript position
   659→	currentPos, err := analyzer.GetTranscriptPosition(state.TranscriptPath)
   660→	if err != nil {
   661→		return false, nil //nolint:nilerr // Error reading transcript, fail gracefully
   662→	}
   663→
   664→	// Check if transcript has grown since last condensation
   665→	if currentPos <= state.CondensedTranscriptLines {
   666→		return false, nil // No new content
   667→	}
   668→
   669→	// Transcript has grown - check if there are file modifications in the new portion
   670→	modifiedFiles, _, err := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(state.TranscriptPath, state.CondensedTranscriptLines)
   671→	if err != nil {
   672→		return false, nil //nolint:nilerr // Error parsing transcript, fail gracefully
   673→	}
   674→
   675→	// No file modifications means no new content to checkpoint
   676→	if len(modifiedFiles) == 0 {
   677→		return false, nil
   678→	}
   679→
   680→	// Check if any modified files overlap with currently staged files
   681→	// This ensures we only add checkpoint trailers to commits that include
   682→	// files the agent actually modified
   683→	stagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo) // TODO: does it work? Do we have staged files if this is called at post-commit hook ?
   684→	if !hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, modifiedFiles) {
   685→		return false, nil // No overlap - staged files are unrelated to agent's work
   686→	}
   687→
   688→	return true, nil
   689→}
   690→
   691→// addCheckpointTrailer adds the Entire-Checkpoint trailer to a commit message.
   692→// Handles proper trailer formatting (blank line before trailers if needed).
   693→func addCheckpointTrailer(message string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) string {
   694→	trailer := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
   695→
   696→	// If message already ends with trailers (lines starting with key:), just append
   697→	// Otherwise, add a blank line first
   698→	lines := strings.Split(strings.TrimRight(message, "\n"), "\n")
   699→
   700→	// Check if last non-empty, non-comment line looks like a trailer
   701→	// Git comment lines start with # and may contain ": " (e.g., "# Changes to be committed:")
   702→	hasTrailers := false
   703→	for i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   704→		line := strings.TrimSpace(lines[i])
   705→		if line == "" {
   706→			break
   707→		}
   708→		// Skip git comment lines
   709→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "#") {
   710→			continue
   711→		}
   712→		if strings.Contains(line, ": ") {
   713→			hasTrailers = true
   714→			break
   715→		}
   716→		// Non-comment, non-trailer line found - no existing trailers
   717→		break
   718→	}
   719→
   720→	if hasTrailers {
   721→		// Append trailer directly
   722→		return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n" + trailer + "\n"
   723→	}
   724→
   725→	// Add blank line before trailer
   726→	return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n"
   727→}
   728→
   729→// addCheckpointTrailerWithComment adds the Entire-Checkpoint trailer with an explanatory comment.
   730→// The trailer is placed above the git comment block but below the user's message area,
   731→// with a comment explaining that the user can remove it if they don't want to link the commit
   732→// to the agent session. If prompt is non-empty, it's shown as context.
   733→func addCheckpointTrailerWithComment(message string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, agentName, prompt string) string {
   734→	trailer := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
   735→	commentLines := []string{
   736→		"# Remove the Entire-Checkpoint trailer above if you don't want to link this commit to " + agentName + " session context.",
   737→	}
   738→	if prompt != "" {
   739→		commentLines = append(commentLines, "# Last Prompt: "+prompt)
   740→	}
   741→	commentLines = append(commentLines, "# The trailer will be added to your next commit based on this branch.")
   742→	comment := strings.Join(commentLines, "\n")
   743→
   744→	lines := strings.Split(message, "\n")
   745→
   746→	// Find where the git comment block starts (first # line)
   747→	commentStart := -1
   748→	for i, line := range lines {
   749→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "#") {
   750→			commentStart = i
   751→			break
   752→		}
   753→	}
   754→
   755→	if commentStart == -1 {
   756→		// No git comments, append trailer at the end
   757→		return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n"
   758→	}
   759→
   760→	// Split into user content and git comments
   761→	userContent := strings.Join(lines[:commentStart], "\n")
   762→	gitComments := strings.Join(lines[commentStart:], "\n")
   763→
   764→	// Build result: user content, blank line, trailer, comment, blank line, git comments
   765→	userContent = strings.TrimRight(userContent, "\n")
   766→	if userContent == "" {
   767→		// No user content yet - leave space for them to type, then trailer
   768→		// Two newlines: first for user's message line, second for blank separator
   769→		return "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n\n" + gitComments
   770→	}
   771→	return userContent + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n\n" + gitComments
   772→}
   773→
   774→// InitializeSession creates session state for a new session or updates an existing one.
   775→// This implements the optional SessionInitializer interface.
   776→// Called during UserPromptSubmit to allow git hooks to detect active sessions.
   777→//
   778→// If the session already exists and HEAD has moved (e.g., user committed), updates
   779→// BaseCommit to the new HEAD so future checkpoints go to the correct shadow branch.
   780→//
   781→// If there's an existing shadow branch with commits from a different session ID,
   782→// returns a SessionIDConflictError to prevent orphaning existing session work.
   783→//
   784→// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
   785→// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
   786→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) InitializeSession(sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string) error {
   787→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   788→	if err != nil {
   789→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   790→	}
   791→
   792→	// Get current HEAD
   793→	head, err := repo.Head()
   794→	if err != nil {
   795→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   796→	}
   797→
   798→	// Check if session already exists
   799→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   800→	if err != nil {
   801→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check session state: %w", err)
   802→	}
   803→
   804→	if state != nil && state.BaseCommit != "" {
   805→		// Session is fully initialized
   806→		needSave := false
   807→
   808→		// Backfill AgentType if empty (for sessions created before the agent_type field was added)
   809→		if state.AgentType == "" && agentType != "" {
   810→			state.AgentType = agentType
   811→			needSave = true
   812→		}
   813→
   814→		// Update transcript path if provided (may change on session resume)
   815→		if transcriptPath != "" && state.TranscriptPath != transcriptPath {
   816→			state.TranscriptPath = transcriptPath
   817→			needSave = true
   818→		}
   819→
   820→		// Clear LastCheckpointID on every new prompt
   821→		// This is set during PostCommit when a checkpoint is created, and should be
   822→		// cleared when the user enters a new prompt (starting fresh work)
   823→		if state.LastCheckpointID != "" {
   824→			state.LastCheckpointID = ""
   825→			needSave = true
   826→		}
   827→
   828→		// Calculate attribution at prompt start (BEFORE agent makes any changes)
   829→		// This captures user edits since the last checkpoint (or base commit for first prompt).
   830→		// IMPORTANT: Always calculate attribution, even for the first checkpoint, to capture
   831→		// user edits made before the first prompt. The inner CalculatePromptAttribution handles
   832→		// nil lastCheckpointTree by falling back to baseTree.
   833→		promptAttr := s.calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(repo, state)
   834→		state.PendingPromptAttribution = &promptAttr
   835→		needSave = true
   836→
   837→		// Check if HEAD has moved (user pulled/rebased or committed)
   838→		if state.BaseCommit != head.Hash().String() {
   839→			oldBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
   840→			newBaseCommit := head.Hash().String()
   841→
   842→			// Check if old shadow branch exists - if so, user did NOT commit (would have been deleted)
   843→			// This happens when user does: stash → pull → stash apply, or rebase, etc.
   844→			oldShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(oldBaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   845→			oldRefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(oldShadowBranch)
   846→			if oldRef, err := repo.Reference(oldRefName, true); err == nil {
   847→				// Old shadow branch exists - move it to new base commit
   848→				newShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(newBaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   849→				newRefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(newShadowBranch)
   850→
   851→				// Create new reference pointing to same commit
   852→				newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(newRefName, oldRef.Hash())
   853→				if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
   854→					return fmt.Errorf("failed to create new shadow branch %s: %w", newShadowBranch, err)
   855→				}
   856→
   857→				// Delete old reference
   858→				if err := repo.Storer.RemoveReference(oldRefName); err != nil {
   859→					// Non-fatal: log but continue
   860→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to remove old shadow branch %s: %v\n", oldShadowBranch, err)
   861→				}
   862→
   863→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Moved shadow branch from %s to %s (base commit changed after pull/rebase)\n",
   864→					oldShadowBranch, newShadowBranch)
   865→			}
   866→
   867→			state.BaseCommit = newBaseCommit
   868→			needSave = true
   869→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session base commit to %s\n", newBaseCommit[:7])
   870→		}
   871→
   872→		if needSave {
   873→			if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   874→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to update session state: %w", err)
   875→			}
   876→		}
   877→		return nil
   878→	}
   879→	// If state exists but BaseCommit is empty, it's a partial state from concurrent warning
   880→	// Continue below to properly initialize it
   881→
   882→	// Initialize new session
   883→	state, err = s.initializeSession(repo, sessionID, agentType, transcriptPath)
   884→	if err != nil {
   885→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to initialize session: %w", err)
   886→	}
   887→
   888→	// Calculate attribution for pre-prompt edits
   889→	// This captures any user edits made before the first prompt
   890→	promptAttr := s.calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(repo, state)
   891→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = &promptAttr
   892→	if err = s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   893→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save attribution: %w", err)
   894→	}
   895→
   896→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Initialized shadow session: %s\n", sessionID)
   897→	return nil
   898→}
   899→
   900→// calculatePromptAttributionAtStart calculates attribution at prompt start (before agent runs).
   901→// This captures user changes since the last checkpoint - no filtering needed since
   902→// the agent hasn't made any changes yet.
   903→//
   904→// IMPORTANT: This reads from the worktree (not staging area) to match what WriteTemporary
   905→// captures in checkpoints. If we read staged content but checkpoints capture worktree content,
   906→// unstaged changes would be in the checkpoint but not counted in PromptAttribution, causing
   907→// them to be incorrectly attributed to the agent later.
   908→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(
   909→	repo *git.Repository,
   910→	state *SessionState,
   911→) PromptAttribution {
   912→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
   913→	nextCheckpointNum := state.CheckpointCount + 1
   914→	result := PromptAttribution{CheckpointNumber: nextCheckpointNum}
   915→
   916→	// Get last checkpoint tree from shadow branch (if it exists)
   917→	// For the first checkpoint, no shadow branch exists yet - this is fine,
   918→	// CalculatePromptAttribution will use baseTree as the reference instead.
   919→	var lastCheckpointTree *object.Tree
   920→	shadowBranchName := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   921→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   922→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   923→	if err != nil {
   924→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: no shadow branch yet (first checkpoint)",
   925→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName))
   926→		// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
   927→	} else {
   928→		shadowCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   929→		if err != nil {
   930→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: failed to get shadow commit",
   931→				slog.String("shadow_ref", ref.Hash().String()),
   932→				slog.String("error", err.Error()))
   933→			// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
   934→		} else {
   935→			lastCheckpointTree, err = shadowCommit.Tree()
   936→			if err != nil {
   937→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: failed to get shadow tree",
   938→					slog.String("error", err.Error()))
   939→				// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
   940→			}
   941→		}
   942→	}
   943→
   944→	// Get base tree for agent lines calculation
   945→	var baseTree *object.Tree
   946→	if baseCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(plumbing.NewHash(state.BaseCommit)); err == nil {
   947→		if tree, treeErr := baseCommit.Tree(); treeErr == nil {
   948→			baseTree = tree
   949→		} else {
   950→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: base tree unavailable",
   951→				slog.String("error", treeErr.Error()))
   952→		}
   953→	} else {
   954→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: base commit unavailable",
   955→			slog.String("base_commit", state.BaseCommit),
   956→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
   957→	}
   958→
   959→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   960→	if err != nil {
   961→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution skipped: failed to get worktree",
   962→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
   963→		return result
   964→	}
   965→
   966→	// Get worktree status to find ALL changed files
   967→	status, err := worktree.Status()
   968→	if err != nil {
   969→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution skipped: failed to get worktree status",
   970→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
   971→		return result
   972→	}
   973→
   974→	worktreeRoot := worktree.Filesystem.Root()
   975→
   976→	// Build map of changed files with their worktree content
   977→	// IMPORTANT: We read from worktree (not staging area) to match what WriteTemporary
   978→	// captures in checkpoints. This ensures attribution is consistent.
   979→	changedFiles := make(map[string]string)
   980→	for filePath, fileStatus := range status {
   981→		// Skip unmodified files
   982→		if fileStatus.Worktree == git.Unmodified && fileStatus.Staging == git.Unmodified {
   983→			continue
   984→		}
   985→		// Skip .entire metadata directory (session data, not user code)
   986→		if strings.HasPrefix(filePath, paths.EntireMetadataDir+"/") || strings.HasPrefix(filePath, ".entire/") {
   987→			continue
   988→		}
   989→
   990→		// Always read from worktree to match checkpoint behavior
   991→		fullPath := filepath.Join(worktreeRoot, filePath)
   992→		var content string
   993→		if data, err := os.ReadFile(fullPath); err == nil { //nolint:gosec // filePath is from git worktree status
   994→			// Use git's binary detection algorithm (matches getFileContent behavior).
   995→			// Binary files are excluded from line-based attribution calculations.
   996→			isBinary, binErr := binary.IsBinary(bytes.NewReader(data))
   997→			if binErr == nil && !isBinary {
   998→				content = string(data)
   999→			}
  1000→		}
  1001→		// else: file deleted, unreadable, or binary - content remains empty string
  1002→
  1003→		changedFiles[filePath] = content
  1004→	}
  1005→
  1006→	// Use CalculatePromptAttribution from manual_commit_attribution.go
  1007→	result = CalculatePromptAttribution(baseTree, lastCheckpointTree, changedFiles, nextCheckpointNum)
  1008→
  1009→	return result
  1010→}
  1011→
  1012→// getStagedFiles returns a list of files staged for commit.
  1013→func getStagedFiles(repo *git.Repository) []string {
  1014→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
  1015→	if err != nil {
  1016→		return nil
  1017→	}
  1018→
  1019→	status, err := worktree.Status()
  1020→	if err != nil {
  1021→		return nil
  1022→	}
  1023→
  1024→	var staged []string
  1025→	for path, fileStatus := range status {
  1026→		// Check if file is staged (in index)
  1027→		if fileStatus.Staging != git.Unmodified && fileStatus.Staging != git.Untracked {
  1028→			staged = append(staged, path)
  1029→		}
  1030→	}
  1031→	return staged
  1032→}
  1033→
  1034→// getLastPrompt retrieves the most recent user prompt from a session's shadow branch.
  1035→// Returns empty string if no prompt can be retrieved.
  1036→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getLastPrompt(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) string {
  1037→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
  1038→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
  1039→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1040→	if err != nil {
  1041→		return ""
  1042→	}
  1043→
  1044→	// Extract session data to get prompts for commit message generation
  1045→	// Pass agent type to handle different transcript formats (JSONL for Claude, JSON for Gemini)
  1046→	sessionData, err := s.extractSessionData(repo, ref.Hash(), state.SessionID, nil, state.AgentType)
  1047→	if err != nil || len(sessionData.Prompts) == 0 {
  1048→		return ""
  1049→	}
  1050→
  1051→	// Return the last prompt (most recent work before commit)
  1052→	return sessionData.Prompts[len(sessionData.Prompts)-1]
  1053→}
  1054→
  1055→// hasOverlappingFiles checks if any file in stagedFiles appears in filesTouched.
  1056→func hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, filesTouched []string) bool {
  1057→	touchedSet := make(map[string]bool)
  1058→	for _, f := range filesTouched {
  1059→		touchedSet[f] = true
  1060→	}
  1061→
  1062→	for _, staged := range stagedFiles {
  1063→		if touchedSet[staged] {
  1064→			return true
  1065→		}
  1066→	}
  1067→	return false
  1068→}
  1069→

    19→// loadSessionState loads session state using the StateStore.
    20→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) loadSessionState(sessionID string) (*SessionState, error) {
    21→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    22→	if err != nil {
    23→		return nil, err
    24→	}
    25→	state, err := store.Load(context.Background(), sessionID)
    26→	if err != nil {

    29→	return sessionStateToStrategy(state), nil
    30→}

    32→// saveSessionState saves session state using the StateStore.
    33→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) saveSessionState(state *SessionState) error {
    34→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    35→	if err != nil {
    36→		return err
    37→	}
    38→	if err := store.Save(context.Background(), sessionStateFromStrategy(state)); err != nil {
    39→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
    40→	}
    41→	return nil
    42→}
    43→
    44→// clearSessionState clears session state using the StateStore.
    45→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) clearSessionState(sessionID string) error {
    46→	store, err := s.getStateStore()

    48→		return err
    49→	}

    52→	}
    53→	return nil
    54→}
    55→
    56→// listAllSessionStates returns all active session states.
    57→// It filters out orphaned sessions whose shadow branch no longer exists.
    58→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) listAllSessionStates() ([]*SessionState, error) {
    59→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    60→	if err != nil {
    61→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get state store: %w", err)
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	sessionStates, err := store.List(context.Background())
    65→	if err != nil {
    66→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	if len(sessionStates) == 0 {
    70→		return nil, nil
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    74→	if err != nil {
    75→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	var states []*SessionState
    79→	for _, sessionState := range sessionStates {
    80→		state := sessionStateToStrategy(sessionState)
    81→
    82→		// Skip and cleanup orphaned sessions whose shadow branch no longer exists
    83→		// Only cleanup if the session has created checkpoints (CheckpointCount > 0)
    84→		// AND has no LastCheckpointID (not recently condensed)
    85→		// Sessions with LastCheckpointID are valid - they were condensed and the shadow
    86→		// branch was intentionally deleted. Keep them for LastCheckpointID reuse.
    87→		shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    88→		refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
    89→		if _, err := repo.Reference(refName, true); err != nil {
    90→			// Shadow branch doesn't exist
    91→			// Only cleanup if session has checkpoints AND no LastCheckpointID
    92→			// Sessions with LastCheckpointID should be kept for checkpoint reuse
    93→			if state.CheckpointCount > 0 && state.LastCheckpointID == "" {
    94→				// Clear the orphaned session state (best-effort, don't fail listing)
    95→				//nolint:errcheck,gosec // G104: Cleanup is best-effort, shouldn't fail the list operation
    96→				store.Clear(context.Background(), state.SessionID)
    97→				continue
    98→			}
    99→			// Keep sessions with LastCheckpointID or no checkpoints yet
   100→		}
   101→
   102→		states = append(states, state)
   103→	}
   104→	return states, nil
   105→}

   107→// findSessionsForWorktree finds all sessions for the given worktree path.
   108→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath string) ([]*SessionState, error) {
   109→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   110→	if err != nil {
   111→		return nil, err
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	var matching []*SessionState
   115→	for _, state := range allStates {
   116→		if state.WorktreePath == worktreePath {
   117→			matching = append(matching, state)
   118→		}
   119→	}
   120→	return matching, nil
   121→}
   122→

   124→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) findSessionsForCommit(baseCommitSHA string) ([]*SessionState, error) {
   125→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   126→	if err != nil {
   127→		return nil, err
   128→	}
   129→
   130→	var matching []*SessionState
   131→	for _, state := range allStates {
   132→		if state.BaseCommit == baseCommitSHA {
   133→			matching = append(matching, state)
   134→		}
   135→	}
   136→	return matching, nil
   137→}

   140→// Used by the rewind reset command to find sessions to clean up.
   141→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) FindSessionsForCommit(baseCommitSHA string) ([]*SessionState, error) {
   142→	return s.findSessionsForCommit(baseCommitSHA)
   143→}

   149→}
   150→
   151→// HasOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints checks if there are other active sessions
   152→// from the SAME worktree (different from currentSessionID) that have created checkpoints
   153→// on the SAME base commit (current HEAD). This is used to detect concurrent sessions
   154→// in different terminals but same directory.
   155→// Returns the first found session with CheckpointCount > 0, or nil if none found.
   156→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) HasOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(currentSessionID string) (*SessionState, error) {
   157→	currentWorktree, err := GetWorktreePath()
   158→	if err != nil {
   159→		return nil, err
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	// Get current HEAD to compare with session base commits
   163→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   164→	if err != nil {
   165→		return nil, err
   166→	}
   167→	head, err := repo.Head()
   168→	if err != nil {
   169→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   170→	}
   171→	currentHead := head.Hash().String()
   172→
   173→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   174→	if err != nil {
   175→		return nil, err
   176→	}
   177→
   178→	for _, state := range allStates {
   179→		// Only consider sessions from the same worktree with checkpoints
   180→		// AND based on the same commit (current HEAD)
   181→		// Sessions from different base commits are independent and shouldn't trigger warning
   182→		if state.SessionID != currentSessionID &&
   183→			state.WorktreePath == currentWorktree &&
   184→			state.CheckpointCount > 0 &&
   185→			state.BaseCommit == currentHead {
   186→			return state, nil
   187→		}
   188→	}
   189→	return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil,nil indicates no other session found (expected case)
   190→}
   191→
   192→// initializeSession creates a new session state or updates a partial one.
   193→// A partial state may exist if the concurrent session warning was shown.
   194→// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
   195→// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
   196→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) initializeSession(repo *git.Repository, sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string) (*SessionState, error) {
   197→	head, err := repo.Head()
   198→	if err != nil {
   199→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   200→	}
   201→
   202→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   203→	if err != nil {
   204→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree path: %w", err)
   205→	}
   206→
   207→	// Get worktree ID for shadow branch naming
   208→	worktreeID, err := paths.GetWorktreeID(worktreePath)
   209→	if err != nil {
   210→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree ID: %w", err)
   211→	}
   212→
   213→	// Capture untracked files at session start to preserve them during rewind
   214→	untrackedFiles, err := collectUntrackedFiles()
   215→	if err != nil {
   216→		// Non-fatal: continue even if we can't collect untracked files
   217→		untrackedFiles = nil
   218→	}
   219→
   220→	state := &SessionState{
   221→		SessionID:             sessionID,
   222→		BaseCommit:            head.Hash().String(),
   223→		WorktreePath:          worktreePath,
   224→		WorktreeID:            worktreeID,
   225→		StartedAt:             time.Now(),
   226→		CheckpointCount:       0,
   227→		UntrackedFilesAtStart: untrackedFiles,
   228→		AgentType:             agentType,
   229→		TranscriptPath:        transcriptPath,
   230→	}
   231→
   232→	if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   233→		return nil, err
   234→	}
   235→
   236→	return state, nil
   237→}

   241→func getShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit, worktreeID string) string {
   242→	return checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit, worktreeID)
   243→}

   900→// InitializeSession creates session state for a new session.
   901→// This is called during UserPromptSubmit hook to set up tracking for the session.
   902→// For auto-commit strategy, this creates a SessionState file in .git/entire-sessions/
   903→// to track CondensedTranscriptLines (transcript offset) across checkpoints.
   904→// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
   905→// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
   906→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) InitializeSession(sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string) error {
   907→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   908→	if err != nil {
   909→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   910→	}
   911→
   912→	// Get current HEAD commit to track as base

   915→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   916→	}
   917→
   918→	baseCommit := head.Hash().String()
   919→

   921→	existing, err := LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   922→	if err != nil {
   923→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check existing session state: %w", err)
   924→	}
   925→	if existing != nil {
   926→		// Session already initialized, nothing to do
   927→		return nil
   928→	}
   929→
   930→	// Create new session state

   932→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   933→		BaseCommit:               baseCommit,
   934→		StartedAt:                time.Now(),
   935→		CheckpointCount:          0,
   936→		CondensedTranscriptLines: 0, // Start from beginning of transcript
   937→		FilesTouched:             []string{},
   938→		AgentType:                agentType,
   939→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptPath,
   940→	}
   941→
   942→	if err := SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   943→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   944→	}
   945→
   946→	return nil
   947→}

    56→		Long: `Enable Entire with session tracking for your AI agent workflows.
    57→
    58→Uses the manual-commit strategy by default. To use a different strategy:

    60→  entire enable --strategy auto-commit
    61→
    62→Strategies: manual-commit (default), auto-commit`,

    64→			// Check if we're in a git repository first - this is a prerequisite error,
    65→			// not a usage error, so we silence Cobra's output and use SilentError
    66→			// to prevent duplicate error output in main.go

    69→				cmd.SilenceErrors = true
