```
    73→
    74→			if err := validateSetupFlags(useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings); err != nil {
    75→				return err
    76→			}
    77→			// Non-interactive mode if --agent flag is provided
    78→			if agentName != "" {
    79→				return setupAgentHooksNonInteractive(agent.AgentName(agentName), strategyFlag, localDev, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry, disableMultisessionWarning)
    80→			}
    81→			// If strategy is specified via flag, skip interactive selection
    82→			if strategyFlag != "" {
    83→				return runEnableWithStrategy(cmd.OutOrStdout(), strategyFlag, localDev, ignoreUntracked, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, setupShell, skipPushSessions, telemetry, disableMultisessionWarning)
    84→			}
    85→			return runEnableInteractive(cmd.OutOrStdout(), localDev, ignoreUntracked, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, setupShell, skipPushSessions, telemetry, disableMultisessionWarning)
    86→		},
    87→	}
    88→
    89→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&localDev, "local-dev", false, "Use go run instead of entire binary for hooks")
    90→	cmd.Flags().MarkHidden("local-dev") //nolint:errcheck,gosec // flag is defined above
    91→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&ignoreUntracked, "ignore-untracked", false, "Commit all new files without tracking pre-existing untracked files")
    92→	cmd.Flags().MarkHidden("ignore-untracked") //nolint:errcheck,gosec // flag is defined above
    93→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&useLocalSettings, "local", false, "Write settings to settings.local.json instead of settings.json")
    94→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&useProjectSettings, "project", false, "Write settings to settings.json even if it already exists")
    95→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&agentName, "agent", "", "Agent to setup hooks for (e.g., claude-code). Enables non-interactive mode.")
    96→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&strategyFlag, "strategy", "", "Strategy to use (manual-commit or auto-commit)")
    97→	cmd.Flags().BoolVarP(&forceHooks, "force", "f", false, "Force reinstall hooks (removes existing Entire hooks first)")
    98→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&setupShell, "setup-shell", false, "Add shell completion to your rc file (non-interactive)")
    99→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&skipPushSessions, "skip-push-sessions", false, "Disable automatic pushing of session logs on git push")
   100→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&disableMultisessionWarning, "disable-multisession-warning", false, "Disable warnings when multiple sessions are active on the same commit")
   101→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&telemetry, "telemetry", true, "Enable anonymous usage analytics")
   102→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // completion is optional, flag is defined above
   103→	cmd.RegisterFlagCompletionFunc("strategy", func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string, _ string) ([]string, cobra.ShellCompDirective) {
   104→		return []string{strategyDisplayManualCommit, strategyDisplayAutoCommit}, cobra.ShellCompDirectiveNoFileComp
   105→	})
   106→
   107→	// Add subcommands for automation/testing
   108→	cmd.AddCommand(newSetupGitHookCmd())
   109→
   110→	return cmd
   111→}
   112→
   113→func newDisableCmd() *cobra.Command {
   114→	var useProjectSettings bool
   115→	var uninstall bool
   116→	var force bool
   117→
   118→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   119→		Use:   "disable",
   120→		Short: "Disable Entire",
   121→		Long: `Disable Entire temporarily. Hooks will exit silently and commands will show a disabled message.
   122→
   123→Use --uninstall to completely remove Entire from this repository, including:
   124→  - .entire/ directory (settings, logs, metadata)
   125→  - Git hooks (prepare-commit-msg, commit-msg, post-commit, pre-push)
   126→  - Session state files (.git/entire-sessions/)
   127→  - Shadow branches (entire/<hash>)
   128→  - Agent hooks (Claude Code, Gemini CLI)`,
   129→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   130→			if uninstall {
   131→				return runUninstall(cmd.OutOrStdout(), cmd.ErrOrStderr(), force)
   132→			}
   133→			return runDisable(cmd.OutOrStdout(), useProjectSettings)
   134→		},
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&useProjectSettings, "project", false, "Update settings.json instead of settings.local.json")
   138→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&uninstall, "uninstall", false, "Completely remove Entire from this repository")
   139→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&force, "force", false, "Skip confirmation prompt (use with --uninstall)")
   140→
   141→	return cmd
   142→}
   143→
   144→func newStatusCmd() *cobra.Command {
   145→	var detailed bool
   146→
   147→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   148→		Use:   "status",
   149→		Short: "Show Entire status",
   150→		Long:  "Show whether Entire is currently enabled or disabled",
   151→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   152→			return runStatus(cmd.OutOrStdout(), detailed)
   153→		},
   154→	}
   155→
   156→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&detailed, "detailed", false, "Show detailed status for each settings file")
   157→
   158→	return cmd
   159→}
   160→
   161→// runEnableWithStrategy enables Entire with a specified strategy (non-interactive).
   162→// The selectedStrategy can be either a display name (manual-commit, auto-commit)
   163→// or an internal name (manual-commit, auto-commit).
   164→func runEnableWithStrategy(w io.Writer, selectedStrategy string, localDev, _, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, setupShell, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool, disableMultisessionWarning bool) error {
   165→	// Map the strategy to internal name if it's a display name
   166→	internalStrategy := selectedStrategy
   167→	if mapped, ok := strategyDisplayToInternal[selectedStrategy]; ok {
   168→		internalStrategy = mapped
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	// Validate the strategy exists
   172→	strat, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy)
   173→	if err != nil {
   174→		return fmt.Errorf("unknown strategy: %s (use manual-commit or auto-commit)", selectedStrategy)
   175→	}
   176→
   177→	// Setup Claude Code hooks
   178→	hooksInstalled, err := setupClaudeCodeHook(localDev, forceHooks)
   179→	if err != nil {
   180→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup Claude Code hooks: %w", err)
   181→	}
   182→	if hooksInstalled > 0 {
   183→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Claude Code hooks installed")
   184→	} else {
   185→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Claude Code hooks verified")
   186→	}
   187→
   188→	// Setup .entire directory
   189→	dirCreated, err := setupEntireDirectory()
   190→	if err != nil {
   191→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .entire directory: %w", err)
   192→	}
   193→	if dirCreated {
   194→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ .entire directory created")
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	// Load existing settings to preserve other options (like strategy_options.push)
   198→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   199→	if err != nil {
   200→		// If we can't load, start with defaults
   201→		settings = &EntireSettings{}
   202→	}
   203→	// Update the specific fields
   204→	settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   205→	settings.LocalDev = localDev
   206→	settings.Enabled = true
   207→
   208→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   209→	if skipPushSessions {
   210→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   211→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   212→		}
   213→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	// Set disable_multisession_warning option if --disable-multisession-warning flag was provided
   217→	if disableMultisessionWarning {
   218→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   219→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   220→		}
   221→		settings.StrategyOptions["disable_multisession_warning"] = true
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	// Handle telemetry for non-interactive mode
   225→	// Note: if telemetry is nil (not configured), it defaults to disabled
   226→	if !telemetry || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   227→		f := false
   228→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   229→	}
   230→
   231→	// Determine which settings file to write to
   232→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   233→	if err != nil {
   234→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   235→	}
   236→	shouldUseLocal, showNotification := determineSettingsTarget(entireDirAbs, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings)
   237→
   238→	if showNotification {
   239→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Info: Project settings exist. Saving to settings.local.json instead.")
   240→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Use --project to update the project settings file.")
   241→	}
   242→
   243→	if shouldUseLocal {
   244→		if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings); err != nil {
   245→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
   246→		}
   247→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Local settings saved (.entire/settings.local.json)")
   248→	} else {
   249→		if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   250→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   251→		}
   252→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Project settings saved (.entire/settings.json)")
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	// Install git hooks (always reinstall to ensure they're up-to-date)
   256→	gitHooksInstalled, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true)
   257→	if err != nil {
   258→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   259→	}
   260→	if gitHooksInstalled > 0 {
   261→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Git hooks installed")
   262→	} else {
   263→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Git hooks verified")
   264→	}
   265→
   266→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements
   267→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   268→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   269→	}
   270→
   271→	// Setup shell completion if --setup-shell flag was provided
   272→	if setupShell {
   273→		if err := setupShellCompletionNonInteractive(w); err != nil {
   274→			// Non-fatal - just log and continue
   275→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "Note: Shell completion setup skipped: %v\n", err)
   276→		}
   277→	}
   278→
   279→	// Show success message with display name
   280→	displayName := selectedStrategy
   281→	if dn, ok := strategyInternalToDisplay[internalStrategy]; ok {
   282→		displayName = dn
   283→	}
   284→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "\n✓ %s strategy enabled\n", displayName)
   285→
   286→	return nil
   287→}
   288→
   289→// runEnableInteractive runs the interactive enable flow.
   290→func runEnableInteractive(w io.Writer, localDev, _, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, setupShell, skipPushSessions, telemetry, disableMultisessionWarning bool) error {
   291→	// Use the default strategy (manual-commit)
   292→	internalStrategy := strategy.DefaultStrategyName
   293→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Using %s strategy (use --strategy to change)\n\n", strategyInternalToDisplay[internalStrategy])
   294→
   295→	// Setup Claude Code hooks
   296→	hooksInstalled, err := setupClaudeCodeHook(localDev, forceHooks)
   297→	if err != nil {
   298→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup Claude Code hooks: %w", err)
   299→	}
   300→	if hooksInstalled > 0 {
   301→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Claude Code hooks installed")
   302→	} else {
   303→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Claude Code hooks verified")
   304→	}
   305→
   306→	// Setup .entire directory
   307→	dirCreated, err := setupEntireDirectory()
   308→	if err != nil {
   309→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .entire directory: %w", err)
   310→	}
   311→	if dirCreated {
   312→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ .entire directory created")
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	// Load existing settings to preserve other options (like strategy_options.push)
   316→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   317→	if err != nil {
   318→		// If we can't load, start with defaults
   319→		settings = &EntireSettings{}
   320→	}
   321→	// Update the specific fields
   322→	settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   323→	settings.LocalDev = localDev
   324→	settings.Enabled = true
   325→
   326→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   327→	if skipPushSessions {
   328→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   329→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   330→		}
   331→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   332→	}
   333→
   334→	// Set disable_multisession_warning option if --disable-multisession-warning flag was provided
   335→	if disableMultisessionWarning {
   336→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   337→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   338→		}
   339→		settings.StrategyOptions["disable_multisession_warning"] = true
   340→	}
   341→
   342→	// Ask about telemetry consent (only if not already asked)
   343→	if err := promptTelemetryConsent(settings, telemetry); err != nil {
   344→		return fmt.Errorf("telemetry consent: %w", err)
   345→	}
   346→
   347→	// Determine which settings file to write to (interactive prompt if settings.json exists)
   348→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   349→	if err != nil {
   350→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   351→	}
   352→	shouldUseLocal, err := promptSettingsTarget(entireDirAbs, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings)
   353→	if err != nil {
   354→		return err
   355→	}
   356→
   357→	if shouldUseLocal {
   358→		if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings); err != nil {
   359→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
   360→		}
   361→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Local settings saved (.entire/settings.local.json)")
   362→	} else {
   363→		if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   364→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   365→		}
   366→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Project settings saved (.entire/settings.json)")
   367→	}
   368→
   369→	// Install git hooks (always reinstall to ensure they're up-to-date)
   370→	gitHooksInstalled, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true)
   371→	if err != nil {
   372→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   373→	}
   374→	if gitHooksInstalled > 0 {
   375→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Git hooks installed")
   376→	} else {
   377→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Git hooks verified")
   378→	}
   379→
   380→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements
   381→	strat, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy)
   382→	if err != nil {
   383→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get strategy: %w", err)
   384→	}
   385→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   386→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   387→	}
   388→
   389→	// Setup shell completion - either non-interactively (if --setup-shell) or prompt
   390→	if setupShell {
   391→		if err := setupShellCompletionNonInteractive(w); err != nil {
   392→			// Non-fatal - just log and continue
   393→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "Note: Shell completion setup skipped: %v\n", err)
   394→		}
   395→	} else {
   396→		// Offer to setup shell completion (only if not already configured)
   397→		if err := promptShellCompletion(w); err != nil {
   398→			// Non-fatal - just log and continue
   399→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "Note: Shell completion setup skipped: %v\n", err)
   400→		}
   401→	}
   402→
   403→	// Show success message with display name
   404→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "\n✓ %s strategy enabled\n", strategyInternalToDisplay[internalStrategy])
   405→
   406→	return nil
   407→}
   408→
   409→// runEnable is a simple enable that just sets the enabled flag (for programmatic use).
   410→func runEnable(w io.Writer) error {
   411→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   412→	if err != nil {
   413→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   414→	}
   415→
   416→	settings.Enabled = true
   417→	if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   418→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   419→	}
   420→
   421→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is now enabled.")
   422→	return nil
   423→}
   424→
   425→func runDisable(w io.Writer, useProjectSettings bool) error {
   426→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   427→	if err != nil {
   428→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   429→	}
   430→
   431→	settings.Enabled = false
   432→
   433→	// If --project flag is specified, always write to project settings
   434→	if useProjectSettings {
   435→		if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   436→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   437→		}
   438→	} else {
   439→		// Always write to local settings file (create if doesn't exist)
   440→		if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings); err != nil {
   441→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
   442→		}
   443→	}
   444→
   445→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is now disabled.")
   446→	return nil
   447→}
   448→
   449→func runStatus(w io.Writer, detailed bool) error {
   450→	// Check if we're in a git repository
   451→	if _, repoErr := paths.RepoRoot(); repoErr != nil {
   452→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✕ not a git repository")
   453→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Not being in a git repo is a valid status, not an error
   454→	}
   455→
   456→	// Get absolute paths for settings files
   457→	settingsPath, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsFile)
   458→	if err != nil {
   459→		settingsPath = EntireSettingsFile
   460→	}
   461→	localSettingsPath, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsLocalFile)
   462→	if err != nil {
   463→		localSettingsPath = EntireSettingsLocalFile
   464→	}
   465→
   466→	// Check which settings files exist
   467→	_, projectErr := os.Stat(settingsPath)
   468→	if projectErr != nil && !errors.Is(projectErr, fs.ErrNotExist) {
   469→		return fmt.Errorf("cannot access project settings file: %w", projectErr)
   470→	}
   471→	_, localErr := os.Stat(localSettingsPath)
   472→	if localErr != nil && !errors.Is(localErr, fs.ErrNotExist) {
   473→		return fmt.Errorf("cannot access local settings file: %w", localErr)
   474→	}
   475→	projectExists := projectErr == nil
   476→	localExists := localErr == nil
   477→
   478→	if !projectExists && !localExists {
   479→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "○ not set up (run `entire enable` to get started)")
   480→		return nil
   481→	}
   482→
   483→	if detailed {
   484→		return runStatusDetailed(w, settingsPath, localSettingsPath, projectExists, localExists)
   485→	}
   486→
   487→	// Short output: just show the effective/merged state
   488→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   489→	if err != nil {
   490→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   491→	}
   492→
   493→	fmt.Fprintln(w, formatSettingsStatusShort(settings))
   494→	return nil
   495→}
   496→
   497→// runStatusDetailed shows the effective status plus detailed status for each settings file.
   498→func runStatusDetailed(w io.Writer, settingsPath, localSettingsPath string, projectExists, localExists bool) error {
   499→	// First show the effective/merged status
   500→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   501→	if err != nil {
   502→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   503→	}
   504→	fmt.Fprintln(w, formatSettingsStatusShort(settings))
   505→	fmt.Fprintln(w) // blank line
   506→
   507→	// Show project settings if it exists
   508→	if projectExists {
   509→		projectSettings, err := loadSettingsFromFile(settingsPath)
   510→		if err != nil {
   511→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to load project settings: %w", err)
   512→		}
   513→		fmt.Fprintln(w, formatSettingsStatus("Project", projectSettings))
   514→	}
   515→
   516→	// Show local settings if it exists
   517→	if localExists {
   518→		localSettings, err := loadSettingsFromFile(localSettingsPath)
   519→		if err != nil {
   520→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to load local settings: %w", err)
   521→		}
   522→		fmt.Fprintln(w, formatSettingsStatus("Local", localSettings))
   523→	}
   524→
   525→	return nil
   526→}
   527→
   528→// formatSettingsStatusShort formats a short settings status line.
   529→// Output format: "Enabled (manual-commit)" or "Disabled (auto-commit)"
   530→func formatSettingsStatusShort(settings *EntireSettings) string {
   531→	displayName := settings.Strategy
   532→	if dn, ok := strategyInternalToDisplay[settings.Strategy]; ok {
   533→		displayName = dn
   534→	}
   535→
   536→	if settings.Enabled {
   537→		return fmt.Sprintf("Enabled (%s)", displayName)
   538→	}
   539→	return fmt.Sprintf("Disabled (%s)", displayName)
   540→}
   541→
   542→// formatSettingsStatus formats a settings status line with source prefix.
   543→// Output format: "Project, enabled (manual-commit)" or "Local, disabled (auto-commit)"
   544→func formatSettingsStatus(prefix string, settings *EntireSettings) string {
   545→	displayName := settings.Strategy
   546→	if dn, ok := strategyInternalToDisplay[settings.Strategy]; ok {
   547→		displayName = dn
   548→	}
   549→
   550→	if settings.Enabled {
   551→		return fmt.Sprintf("%s, enabled (%s)", prefix, displayName)
   552→	}
   553→	return fmt.Sprintf("%s, disabled (%s)", prefix, displayName)
   554→}
   555→
   556→// DisabledMessage is the message shown when Entire is disabled
   557→const DisabledMessage = "Entire is disabled. Run `entire enable` to re-enable."
   558→
   559→// checkDisabledGuard checks if Entire is disabled and prints a message if so.
   560→// Returns true if the caller should exit (i.e., Entire is disabled).
   561→// On error reading settings, defaults to enabled (returns false).
   562→func checkDisabledGuard(w io.Writer) bool {
   563→	enabled, err := IsEnabled()
   564→	if err != nil {
   565→		// Default to enabled on error
   566→		return false
   567→	}
   568→	if !enabled {
   569→		fmt.Fprintln(w, DisabledMessage)
   570→		return true
   571→	}
   572→	return false
   573→}
   574→
   575→// setupClaudeCodeHook sets up Claude Code hooks.
   576→// This is a convenience wrapper that uses the agent package.
   577→// Returns the number of hooks installed (0 if already installed).
   578→func setupClaudeCodeHook(localDev, forceHooks bool) (int, error) {
   579→	ag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode)
   580→	if err != nil {
   581→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to get claude-code agent: %w", err)
   582→	}
   583→
   584→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   585→	if !ok {
   586→		return 0, errors.New("claude-code agent does not support hooks")
   587→	}
   588→
   589→	count, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(localDev, forceHooks)
   590→	if err != nil {
   591→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to install claude-code hooks: %w", err)
   592→	}
   593→
   594→	return count, nil
   595→}
   596→
   597→// setupAgentHooksNonInteractive sets up hooks for a specific agent non-interactively.
   598→// If strategyName is provided, it sets the strategy; otherwise uses default.
   599→func setupAgentHooksNonInteractive(agentName agent.AgentName, strategyName string, localDev, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry, disableMultisessionWarning bool) error {
   600→	ag, err := agent.Get(agentName)
   601→	if err != nil {
   602→		return fmt.Errorf("unknown agent: %s", agentName)
   603→	}
   604→
   605→	// Check if agent supports hooks
   606→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   607→	if !ok {
   608→		return fmt.Errorf("agent %s does not support hooks", agentName)
   609→	}
   610→
   611→	// Install hooks
   612→	count, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(localDev, forceHooks)
   613→	if err != nil {
   614→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install hooks for %s: %w", agentName, err)
   615→	}
   616→
   617→	if count == 0 {
   618→		msg := fmt.Sprintf("Hooks for %s already installed", ag.Description())
   619→		if agentName == agent.AgentNameGemini {
   620→			msg += " - This is a work in progress"
   621→		}
   622→		fmt.Println(msg)
   623→	} else {
   624→		msg := fmt.Sprintf("Installed %d hooks for %s", count, ag.Description())
   625→		if agentName == agent.AgentNameGemini {
   626→			msg += " - This is a work in progress"
   627→		}
   628→		fmt.Println(msg)
   629→	}
   630→
   631→	// Update settings to store the strategy
   632→	settings, _ := LoadEntireSettings() //nolint:errcheck // settings defaults are fine
   633→	settings.Enabled = true
   634→	if localDev {
   635→		settings.LocalDev = localDev
   636→	}
   637→
   638→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   639→	if skipPushSessions {
   640→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   641→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   642→		}
   643→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   644→	}
   645→
   646→	// Set disable_multisession_warning option if --disable-multisession-warning flag was provided
   647→	if disableMultisessionWarning {
   648→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   649→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   650→		}
   651→		settings.StrategyOptions["disable_multisession_warning"] = true
   652→	}
   653→
   654→	// Set strategy if provided
   655→	if strategyName != "" {
   656→		// Map display name to internal name if needed
   657→		internalStrategy := strategyName
   658→		if mapped, ok := strategyDisplayToInternal[strategyName]; ok {
   659→			internalStrategy = mapped
   660→		}
   661→		// Validate the strategy exists
   662→		if _, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy); err != nil {
   663→			return fmt.Errorf("unknown strategy: %s (use manual-commit or auto-commit)", strategyName)
   664→		}
   665→		settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   666→	}
   667→
   668→	// Handle telemetry for non-interactive mode
   669→	// Note: if telemetry is nil (not configured), it defaults to disabled
   670→	if !telemetry || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   671→		f := false
   672→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   673→	}
   674→
   675→	if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   676→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   677→	}
   678→
   679→	// Install git hooks (always reinstall to ensure they're up-to-date)
   680→	if _, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   681→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   682→	}
   683→
   684→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements (creates entire/sessions branch, etc.)
   685→	strat, err := strategy.Get(settings.Strategy)
   686→	if err != nil {
   687→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get strategy: %w", err)
   688→	}
   689→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   690→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   691→	}
   692→
   693→	return nil
   694→}
   695→
   696→// validateSetupFlags checks that --local and --project flags are not both specified.
   697→func validateSetupFlags(useLocal, useProject bool) error {
   698→	if useLocal && useProject {
   699→		return errors.New("cannot specify both --project and --local")
   700→	}
   701→	return nil
   702→}
   703→
   704→// determineSettingsTarget decides whether to write to settings.local.json based on:
   705→// - Whether settings.json already exists
   706→// - The --local and --project flags
   707→// Returns (useLocal, showNotification).
   708→func determineSettingsTarget(entireDir string, useLocal, useProject bool) (bool, bool) {
   709→	// Explicit --local flag always uses local settings
   710→	if useLocal {
   711→		return true, false
   712→	}
   713→
   714→	// Explicit --project flag always uses project settings
   715→	if useProject {
   716→		return false, false
   717→	}
   718→
   719→	// No flags specified - check if settings file exists
   720→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(entireDir, paths.SettingsFileName)
   721→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsPath); err == nil {
   722→		// Settings file exists - auto-redirect to local with notification
   723→		return true, true
   724→	}
   725→
   726→	// Settings file doesn't exist - create it
   727→	return false, false
   728→}
   729→
   730→// Settings target options for interactive prompt
   731→const (
   732→	settingsTargetProject = "project"
   733→	settingsTargetLocal   = "local"
   734→)
   735→
   736→// promptSettingsTarget interactively asks the user where to save settings
   737→// when settings.json already exists and no flags were provided.
   738→// Returns (useLocal, error).
   739→func promptSettingsTarget(entireDir string, useLocal, useProject bool) (bool, error) {
   740→	// Explicit --local flag always uses local settings
   741→	if useLocal {
   742→		return true, nil
   743→	}
   744→
   745→	// Explicit --project flag always uses project settings
   746→	if useProject {
   747→		return false, nil
   748→	}
   749→
   750→	// Check if settings file exists
   751→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(entireDir, paths.SettingsFileName)
   752→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsPath); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   753→		// Settings file doesn't exist - create it (no prompt needed)
   754→		return false, nil
   755→	}
   756→
   757→	// Settings file exists - prompt user
   758→	var selected string
   759→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   760→		huh.NewGroup(
   761→			huh.NewSelect[string]().
   762→				Title("Project settings already exist. Where should settings be saved?").
   763→				Options(
   764→					huh.NewOption("Update project settings (settings.json)", settingsTargetProject),
   765→					huh.NewOption("Use local settings (settings.local.json, gitignored)", settingsTargetLocal),
   766→				).
   767→				Value(&selected),
   768→		),
   769→	)
   770→
   771→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   772→		return false, fmt.Errorf("selection cancelled: %w", err)
   773→	}
   774→
   775→	return selected == settingsTargetLocal, nil
   776→}
   777→
   778→// setupEntireDirectory creates the .entire directory and gitignore.
   779→// Returns true if the directory was created, false if it already existed.
   780→func setupEntireDirectory() (bool, error) {
   781→	// Get absolute path for the .entire directory
   782→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   783→	if err != nil {
   784→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   785→	}
   786→
   787→	// Check if directory already exists
   788→	created := false
   789→	if _, err := os.Stat(entireDirAbs); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   790→		created = true
   791→	}
   792→
   793→	// Create .entire directory
   794→	//nolint:gosec // G301: Project directory needs standard permissions for git
   795→	if err := os.MkdirAll(entireDirAbs, 0o755); err != nil {
   796→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to create .entire directory: %w", err)
   797→	}
   798→
   799→	// Create/update .gitignore with all required entries
   800→	if err := strategy.EnsureEntireGitignore(); err != nil {
   801→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .gitignore: %w", err)
   802→	}
   803→
   804→	return created, nil
   805→}

   807→// setupGitHook installs the prepare-commit-msg hook for context trailers.
   808→func setupGitHook() error {
   809→	// Use shared implementation from strategy package
   810→	// The localDev setting is read from settings.json
   811→	_, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(false) // not silent - show output during setup
   812→	if err != nil {
   813→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hook: %w", err)
   814→	}
   815→	return nil
   816→}

   818→// newSetupGitHookCmd creates the standalone git-hook setup command
   819→func newSetupGitHookCmd() *cobra.Command {
   820→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   821→		Use:    "git-hook",
   822→		Short:  "Install git hook for session context trailers",
   823→		Hidden: true, // Hidden as it's mainly for testing
   824→		RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   825→			return setupGitHook()
   826→		},
   827→	}
   828→
   829→	return cmd

   831→
   832→// shellCompletionComment is the comment preceding the completion line
   833→const shellCompletionComment = "# Entire CLI shell completion"
   834→
   835→// promptShellCompletion offers to add shell completion to the user's rc file.
   836→// Only prompts if completion is not already configured.
   837→func promptShellCompletion(w io.Writer) error {
   838→	// Get user's home directory
   839→	home, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   840→	if err != nil {
   841→		//nolint:nilerr // Skip silently if we can't determine home - not a fatal error
   842→		return nil
   843→	}
   844→
   845→	// Determine shell and rc file
   846→	shell := os.Getenv("SHELL")
   847→	var rcFile string
   848→	var completionLine string
   849→
   850→	switch {
   851→	case strings.Contains(shell, "zsh"):
   852→		rcFile = filepath.Join(home, ".zshrc")
   853→		completionLine = "source <(entire completion zsh)"
   854→	case strings.Contains(shell, "bash"):
   855→		rcFile = filepath.Join(home, ".bashrc")
   856→		completionLine = "source <(entire completion bash)"
   857→	default:
   858→		return nil // Unsupported shell, skip silently
   859→	}
   860→
   861→	// Check if completion is already configured
   862→	if isCompletionConfigured(rcFile) {
   863→		return nil // Already configured, skip silently
   864→	}
   865→
   866→	// Prompt user with select-style picker (matching other prompts)
   867→	var selected string
   868→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   869→		huh.NewGroup(
   870→			huh.NewSelect[string]().
   871→				Title("Enable shell completion?").
   872→				Options(
   873→					huh.NewOption("Yes", "yes"),
   874→					huh.NewOption("No", "no"),
   875→				).
   876→				Value(&selected),
   877→		),
   878→	)
   879→
   880→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   881→		//nolint:nilerr // User cancelled - not a fatal error, just skip
   882→		return nil
   883→	}
   884→
   885→	if selected != "yes" {
   886→		return nil
   887→	}
   888→
   889→	// Append completion to rc file
   890→	if err := appendShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine); err != nil {
   891→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to update %s: %w", rcFile, err)
   892→	}
   893→
   894→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Shell completion added to %s\n", rcFile)
   895→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Run `source "+rcFile+"` or restart your shell to activate")
   896→
   897→	return nil
   898→}
   899→
   900→// isCompletionConfigured checks if shell completion is already in the rc file.
   901→func isCompletionConfigured(rcFile string) bool {
   902→	//nolint:gosec // G304: rcFile is constructed from home dir + known filename, not user input
   903→	content, err := os.ReadFile(rcFile)
   904→	if err != nil {
   905→		return false // File doesn't exist or can't read, treat as not configured
   906→	}
   907→	return strings.Contains(string(content), "entire completion")
   908→}
   909→
   910→// appendShellCompletion adds the completion line to the rc file.
   911→func appendShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine string) error {
   912→	//nolint:gosec // G302: Shell rc files need 0644 for user readability
   913→	f, err := os.OpenFile(rcFile, os.O_APPEND|os.O_CREATE|os.O_WRONLY, 0o644)
   914→	if err != nil {
   915→		return fmt.Errorf("opening file: %w", err)
   916→	}
   917→	defer f.Close()
   918→
   919→	// Add newline, comment, and completion line
   920→	_, err = f.WriteString("\n" + shellCompletionComment + "\n" + completionLine + "\n")
   921→	if err != nil {
   922→		return fmt.Errorf("writing completion: %w", err)
   923→	}
   924→	return nil
   925→}
   926→
   927→// setupShellCompletionNonInteractive adds shell completion without prompting.
   928→// Used when --setup-shell flag is provided.
   929→func setupShellCompletionNonInteractive(w io.Writer) error {
   930→	// Get user's home directory
   931→	home, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   932→	if err != nil {
   933→		return fmt.Errorf("cannot determine home directory: %w", err)
   934→	}
   935→
   936→	// Determine shell and rc file
   937→	shell := os.Getenv("SHELL")
   938→	var rcFile string
   939→	var completionLine string
   940→
   941→	switch {
   942→	case strings.Contains(shell, "zsh"):
   943→		rcFile = filepath.Join(home, ".zshrc")
   944→		completionLine = "source <(entire completion zsh)"
   945→	case strings.Contains(shell, "bash"):
   946→		rcFile = filepath.Join(home, ".bashrc")
   947→		completionLine = "source <(entire completion bash)"
   948→	default:
   949→		return fmt.Errorf("unsupported shell: %s (supported: zsh, bash)", shell)
   950→	}
   951→
   952→	// Check if completion is already configured
   953→	if isCompletionConfigured(rcFile) {
   954→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Shell completion already configured in %s\n", rcFile)
   955→		return nil
   956→	}
   957→
   958→	// Append completion to rc file
   959→	if err := appendShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine); err != nil {
   960→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to update %s: %w", rcFile, err)
   961→	}
   962→
   963→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Shell completion added to %s\n", rcFile)
   964→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Run `source "+rcFile+"` or restart your shell to activate")
   965→
   966→	return nil
   967→}
   968→
   969→// promptTelemetryConsent asks the user if they want to enable telemetry.
   970→// It modifies settings.Telemetry based on the user's choice or flags.
   971→// The caller is responsible for saving settings.
   972→func promptTelemetryConsent(settings *EntireSettings, telemetryFlag bool) error {
   973→	// Handle --telemetry=false flag first (always overrides existing setting)
   974→	if !telemetryFlag {
   975→		f := false
   976→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   977→		return nil
   978→	}
   979→
   980→	// Skip if already asked
   981→	if settings.Telemetry != nil {
   982→		return nil
   983→	}
   984→
   985→	// Skip if env var disables telemetry (record as disabled)
   986→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   987→		f := false
   988→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   989→		return nil
   990→	}
   991→
   992→	consent := true // Default to Yes
   993→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   994→		huh.NewGroup(
   995→			huh.NewConfirm().
   996→				Title("Help improve Entire CLI?").
   997→				Description("Share anonymous usage data. No code or personal info collected.").
   998→				Affirmative("Yes").
   999→				Negative("No").
  1000→				Value(&consent),
  1001→		),
  1002→	)
  1003→
  1004→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
  1005→		return fmt.Errorf("telemetry prompt: %w", err)
  1006→	}
  1007→
  1008→	settings.Telemetry = &consent
  1009→	return nil
  1010→}
  1011→
  1012→// runUninstall completely removes Entire from the repository.
  1013→func runUninstall(w, errW io.Writer, force bool) error {
  1014→	// Check if we're in a git repository
  1015→	if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
  1016→		fmt.Fprintln(errW, "Not a git repository. Nothing to uninstall.")
  1017→		return NewSilentError(errors.New("not a git repository"))
  1018→	}
  1019→
  1020→	// Gather counts for display
  1021→	sessionStateCount := countSessionStates()
  1022→	shadowBranchCount := countShadowBranches()
  1023→	gitHooksInstalled := strategy.IsGitHookInstalled()
  1024→	claudeHooksInstalled := checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled()
  1025→	geminiHooksInstalled := checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled()
  1026→	entireDirExists := checkEntireDirExists()
  1027→
  1028→	// Check if there's anything to uninstall
  1029→	if !entireDirExists && !gitHooksInstalled && sessionStateCount == 0 &&
  1030→		shadowBranchCount == 0 && !claudeHooksInstalled && !geminiHooksInstalled {
  1031→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is not installed in this repository.")
  1032→		return nil
  1033→	}
  1034→
  1035→	// Show confirmation prompt unless --force
  1036→	if !force {
  1037→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nThis will completely remove Entire from this repository:")
  1038→		if entireDirExists {
  1039→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - .entire/ directory")
  1040→		}
  1041→		if gitHooksInstalled {
  1042→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Git hooks (prepare-commit-msg, commit-msg, post-commit, pre-push)")
  1043→		}
  1044→		if sessionStateCount > 0 {
  1045→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  - Session state files (%d)\n", sessionStateCount)
  1046→		}
  1047→		if shadowBranchCount > 0 {
  1048→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  - Shadow branches (%d)\n", shadowBranchCount)
  1049→		}
  1050→		switch {
  1051→		case claudeHooksInstalled && geminiHooksInstalled:
  1052→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Claude Code, Gemini CLI)")
  1053→		case claudeHooksInstalled:
  1054→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Claude Code)")
  1055→		case geminiHooksInstalled:
  1056→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Gemini CLI)")
  1057→		}
  1058→		fmt.Fprintln(w)
  1059→
  1060→		var confirmed bool
  1061→		form := NewAccessibleForm(
  1062→			huh.NewGroup(
  1063→				huh.NewConfirm().
  1064→					Title("Are you sure you want to uninstall Entire?").
  1065→					Affirmative("Yes, uninstall").
  1066→					Negative("Cancel").
  1067→					Value(&confirmed),
  1068→			),
  1069→		)
  1070→
  1071→		if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
  1072→			return fmt.Errorf("confirmation cancelled: %w", err)
  1073→		}
  1074→
  1075→		if !confirmed {
  1076→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "Uninstall cancelled.")
  1077→			return nil
  1078→		}
  1079→	}
  1080→
  1081→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nUninstalling Entire CLI...")
  1082→
  1083→	// 1. Remove agent hooks (lowest risk)
  1084→	if err := removeAgentHooks(w); err != nil {
  1085→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove agent hooks: %v\n", err)
  1086→	}
  1087→
  1088→	// 2. Remove git hooks
  1089→	removed, err := strategy.RemoveGitHook()
  1090→	if err != nil {
  1091→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove git hooks: %v\n", err)
  1092→	} else if removed > 0 {
  1093→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed git hooks (%d)\n", removed)
  1094→	}
  1095→
  1096→	// 3. Remove session state files
  1097→	statesRemoved, err := removeAllSessionStates()
  1098→	if err != nil {
  1099→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove session states: %v\n", err)
  1100→	} else if statesRemoved > 0 {
  1101→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed session states (%d)\n", statesRemoved)
  1102→	}
  1103→
  1104→	// 4. Remove .entire/ directory
  1105→	if err := removeEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
  1106→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove .entire directory: %v\n", err)
  1107→	} else if entireDirExists {
  1108→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed .entire directory")
  1109→	}
  1110→
  1111→	// 5. Remove shadow branches
  1112→	branchesRemoved, err := removeAllShadowBranches()
  1113→	if err != nil {
  1114→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove shadow branches: %v\n", err)
  1115→	} else if branchesRemoved > 0 {
  1116→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed %d shadow branches\n", branchesRemoved)
  1117→	}
  1118→
  1119→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nEntire CLI uninstalled successfully.")
  1120→	return nil
  1121→}
  1122→
  1123→// countSessionStates returns the number of active session state files.
  1124→func countSessionStates() int {
  1125→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
  1126→	if err != nil {
  1127→		return 0
  1128→	}
  1129→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
  1130→	if err != nil {
  1131→		return 0
  1132→	}
  1133→	return len(states)
  1134→}
  1135→
  1136→// countShadowBranches returns the number of shadow branches.
  1137→func countShadowBranches() int {
  1138→	branches, err := strategy.ListShadowBranches()
  1139→	if err != nil {
  1140→		return 0
  1141→	}
  1142→	return len(branches)
  1143→}
  1144→
  1145→// checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled checks if Claude Code hooks are installed.
  1146→func checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled() bool {
  1147→	ag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode)
  1148→	if err != nil {
  1149→		return false
  1150→	}
  1151→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
  1152→	if !ok {
  1153→		return false
  1154→	}
  1155→	return hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1156→}
  1157→
  1158→// checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled checks if Gemini CLI hooks are installed.
  1159→func checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled() bool {
  1160→	ag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameGemini)
  1161→	if err != nil {
  1162→		return false
  1163→	}
  1164→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
  1165→	if !ok {
  1166→		return false
  1167→	}
  1168→	return hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1169→}
  1170→
  1171→// checkEntireDirExists checks if the .entire directory exists.
  1172→func checkEntireDirExists() bool {
  1173→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
  1174→	if err != nil {
  1175→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir
  1176→	}
  1177→	_, err = os.Stat(entireDirAbs)
  1178→	return err == nil
  1179→}
  1180→
  1181→// removeAgentHooks removes hooks from all agents that support hooks.
  1182→func removeAgentHooks(w io.Writer) error {
  1183→	var errs []error
  1184→
  1185→	// Remove Claude Code hooks
  1186→	claudeAgent, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode)
  1187→	if err == nil {
  1188→		if hookAgent, ok := claudeAgent.(agent.HookSupport); ok {
  1189→			wasInstalled := hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1190→			if err := hookAgent.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {
  1191→				errs = append(errs, err)
  1192→			} else if wasInstalled {
  1193→				fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed Claude Code hooks")
  1194→			}
  1195→		}
  1196→	}
  1197→
  1198→	// Remove Gemini CLI hooks
  1199→	geminiAgent, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameGemini)
  1200→	if err == nil {
  1201→		if hookAgent, ok := geminiAgent.(agent.HookSupport); ok {
  1202→			wasInstalled := hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1203→			if err := hookAgent.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {
  1204→				errs = append(errs, err)
  1205→			} else if wasInstalled {
  1206→				fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed Gemini CLI hooks")
  1207→			}
  1208→		}
  1209→	}
  1210→
  1211→	return errors.Join(errs...)
  1212→}
  1213→
  1214→// removeAllSessionStates removes all session state files and the directory.
  1215→func removeAllSessionStates() (int, error) {
  1216→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
  1217→	if err != nil {
  1218→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create state store: %w", err)
  1219→	}
  1220→
  1221→	// Count states before removing
  1222→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
  1223→	if err != nil {
  1224→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
  1225→	}
  1226→	count := len(states)
  1227→
  1228→	// Remove the entire directory
  1229→	if err := store.RemoveAll(); err != nil {
  1230→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session states: %w", err)
  1231→	}
  1232→
  1233→	return count, nil
  1234→}
  1235→
  1236→// removeEntireDirectory removes the .entire directory.
  1237→func removeEntireDirectory() error {
  1238→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
  1239→	if err != nil {
  1240→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir
  1241→	}
  1242→	if err := os.RemoveAll(entireDirAbs); err != nil {
  1243→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove .entire directory: %w", err)
  1244→	}
  1245→	return nil
  1246→}
  1247→
  1248→// removeAllShadowBranches removes all shadow branches.
  1249→func removeAllShadowBranches() (int, error) {
  1250→	branches, err := strategy.ListShadowBranches()
  1251→	if err != nil {
  1252→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to list shadow branches: %w", err)
  1253→	}
  1254→	if len(branches) == 0 {
  1255→		return 0, nil
  1256→	}
  1257→	deleted, _, err := strategy.DeleteShadowBranches(branches)
  1258→	return len(deleted), err
  1259→}
```

```
     1→package strategy
```

```
     8→// MaxDescriptionLength is the maximum length for descriptions in commit messages
     9→// before truncation occurs.
    10→const MaxDescriptionLength = 60
    11→
    12→// TruncateDescription truncates a string to maxLen characters, adding "..." if truncated.
    13→// If maxLen is less than 3, returns a string of dots up to maxLen.
    14→func TruncateDescription(s string, maxLen int) string {
    15→	if len(s) <= maxLen {
    16→		return s
    17→	}
    18→	if maxLen < 3 {
    19→		return s[:maxLen]
    20→	}
    21→	return s[:maxLen-3] + "..."
    22→}
    23→
    24→// FormatSubagentEndMessage formats a commit message for when a subagent completes.
    25→// Format: "Completed '<agent-type>' agent: <description> (<tool-use-id>)"
    26→//
    27→// Edge cases:
    28→//   - Empty description: "Completed '<agent-type>' agent (<tool-use-id>)"
    29→//   - Empty agentType: "Completed agent: <description> (<tool-use-id>)"
    30→//   - Both empty: "Task: <tool-use-id>"
```

```json
  "new_string": "\tdeleted, _, err := strategy.DeleteShadowBranches(branches)\n\treturn len(deleted), err\n}\n\n// timeAgo formats a time as a human-readable relative duration.\nfunc timeAgo(t time.Time) string {\n\td := time.Since(t)\n\tswitch {\n\tcase d < time.Minute:\n\t\treturn \"just now\"\n\tcase d < time.Hour:\n\t\tm := int(d.Minutes())\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%dm ago\", m)\n\tcase d < 24*time.Hour:\n\t\th := int(d.Hours())\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%dh ago\", h)\n\tdefault:\n\t\tdays := int(d.Hours() / 24)\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%dd ago\", days)\n\t}\n}\n\n// worktreeGroup groups sessions by worktree path for display.\ntype worktreeGroup struct {\n\tpath     string\n\tbranch   string\n\tsessions []*session.State\n}\n\n// writeActiveSessions writes active session information grouped by worktree.\nfunc writeActiveSessions(w io.Writer) {\n\tstore, err := session.NewStateStore()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tstates, err := store.List(context.Background())\n\tif err != nil || len(states) == 0 {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Filter to active sessions only\n\tvar active []*session.State\n\tfor _, s := range states {\n\t\tif s.EndedAt == nil {\n\t\t\tactive = append(active, s)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif len(active) == 0 {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Group by worktree path\n\tgroups := make(map[string]*worktreeGroup)\n\tfor _, s := range active {\n\t\twp := s.WorktreePath\n\t\tif wp == \"\" {\n\t\t\twp = \"(unknown)\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tg, ok := groups[wp]\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tg = &worktreeGroup{path: wp}\n\t\t\tgroups[wp] = g\n\t\t}\n\t\tg.sessions = append(g.sessions, s)\n\t}\n\n\t// Resolve branch names for each worktree\n\tfor _, g := range groups {\n\t\tg.branch = resolveWorktreeBranch(g.path)\n\t}\n\n\t// Sort groups: alphabetical by path\n\tsortedGroups := make([]*worktreeGroup, 0, len(groups))\n\tfor _, g := range groups {\n\t\tsortedGroups = append(sortedGroups, g)\n\t}\n\tsort.Slice(sortedGroups, func(i, j int) bool {\n\t\treturn sortedGroups[i].path < sortedGroups[j].path\n\t})\n\n\t// Sort sessions within each group by StartedAt (newest first)\n\tfor _, g := range sortedGroups {\n\t\tsort.Slice(g.sessions, func(i, j int) bool {\n\t\t\treturn g.sessions[i].StartedAt.After(g.sessions[j].StartedAt)\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Fprintln(w)\n\tfmt.Fprintln(w, \"Active Sessions:\")\n\tfor _, g := range sortedGroups {\n\t\theader := g.path\n\t\tif g.branch != \"\" {\n\t\t\theader += \" (\" + g.branch + \")\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"  %s\\n\", header)\n\n\t\tfor _, s := range g.sessions {\n\t\t\tshortID := s.SessionID\n\t\t\tif len(shortID) > 7 {\n\t\t\t\tshortID = shortID[:7]\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tprompt := s.FirstPrompt\n\t\t\tif prompt == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tprompt = \"(unknown)\"\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tprompt = stringutil.TruncateRunes(prompt, 40, \"...\")\n\n\t\t\tage := timeAgo(s.StartedAt)\n\n\t\t\tcheckpoints := fmt.Sprintf(\"%d checkpoint\", s.CheckpointCount)\n\t\t\tif s.CheckpointCount != 1 {\n\t\t\t\tcheckpoints += \"s\"\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tuncheckpointed := \"\"\n\t\t\tif s.PendingPromptAttribution != nil {\n\t\t\t\tuncheckpointed = \" (uncheckpointed changes)\"\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"    %-9s \\\"%s\\\"  %s  %s%s\\n\",\n\t\t\t\tshortID, prompt, age, checkpoints, uncheckpointed)\n\t\t}\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(w)\n\t}\n}\n\n// resolveWorktreeBranch resolves the current branch for a worktree path.\nfunc resolveWorktreeBranch(worktreePath string) string {\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), \"git\", \"-C\", worktreePath, \"rev-parse\", \"--abbrev-ref\", \"HEAD\")\n\toutput, err := cmd.Output()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\treturn strings.TrimSpace(string(output))\n}"
}
```

> AGENT

The lint error is that `needSave = true` on line 818 is considered ineffectual because line 842 unconditionally sets it to `true`. However, this is the same pattern used by the other backfills (AgentType on line 812, transcript path on line 824). The lint is technically correct that the assignment at 818 is always overwritten by 842. But the existing code at lines 812 and 824 has the same "issue" — they're all fine because the unconditional set on line 842 is after them.

Wait, the lint is only flagging line 818 specifically. Let me look more carefully... The issue is that `needSave` is set to `true` at line 818, but then at line 842 it's unconditionally set to `true`. So lines 812, 818, 824, and 832 are all "ineffectual" if 842 always runs. But only 818 is flagged — this must be a new-issues-only lint that detects my addition specifically.

The real fix is to not set `needSave = true` redundantly since the attribution code always sets it. But that would change existing code. The cleanest fix for my new code is to simply not set `needSave` in the FirstPrompt block since it's always set below.

All done. Here's a summary of the changes:

### Changes Made

**Step 1: Added `FirstPrompt` field to session state types**
- `cmd/entire/cli/session/state.go` — Added `FirstPrompt string` field to `State` struct
- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_types.go` — Added `FirstPrompt string` field to `SessionState` struct
- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit.go` — Updated `sessionStateToStrategy()` and `sessionStateFromStrategy()` to include `FirstPrompt`

**Step 2: Threaded user prompt through `InitializeSession`**
- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/strategy.go` — Added `userPrompt string` parameter to `SessionInitializer.InitializeSession()` interface
- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` — Updated `InitializeSession()` to accept + backfill `userPrompt`; stores truncated prompt (100 rune-safe chars via `stringutil.TruncateRunes`)
- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_session.go` — Updated `initializeSession()` to accept + store truncated prompt
- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_git.go` — Updated 2 fallback call sites with empty prompt
- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/auto_commit.go` — Updated `InitializeSession()` to accept + store truncated prompt
- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` — Passes `hookData.input.UserPrompt` to `InitializeSession()`
- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_geminicli_handlers.go` — Passes `input.UserPrompt` to `InitializeSession()`

**Step 3: Enhanced `entire status` to show active sessions**
- `cmd/entire/cli/setup.go` — Added:
  - `timeAgo()` helper for human-readable relative timestamps
  - `writeActiveSessions()` to list sessions grouped by worktree with branch name, prompt, age, and checkpoint count
  - `resolveWorktreeBranch()` to get branch name for a worktree path
  - Modified `runStatus()` to call `writeActiveSessions()` when enabled

**Step 4: Tests**
- `cmd/entire/cli/setup_test.go` — Added:
  - `TestTimeAgo` — verifies formatting for just now, minutes, hours, days
  - `TestWriteActiveSessions` — creates mock session states, verifies output format including truncated IDs, prompts, checkpoint counts, and uncheckpointed indicator
  - `TestWriteActiveSessions_NoSessions` — verifies no output when empty
  - `TestWriteActiveSessions_EndedSessionsExcluded` — verifies ended sessions are filtered out
- Updated all existing `InitializeSession` test calls (in `auto_commit_test.go`, `manual_commit_test.go`, `manual_commit_staging_test.go`) with the new 4th parameter

**Verification:** `mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci` all pass cleanly.

> AGENT

I see several issues:

1. **Import ordering** — `os/exec`, `sort`, `time` are in a second group separated by a blank line from the other stdlib imports. `gofmt` won't fix this but it looks wrong and inconsistent. They should be merged with the other stdlib imports.
2. **Variable shadowing** — I'm using `s` as the loop variable name in `writeActiveSessions` which shadows the outer function receiver pattern used elsewhere. Minor but confusing.
3. **`resolveWorktreeBranch` for "(unknown)" path** — If `WorktreePath` is empty and we set it to `"(unknown)"`, we'd then call `git -C (unknown)` which would fail silently. Technically fine (returns empty string), but sloppy — we should skip resolution for unknown paths.
4. **Trailing blank line after last worktree group** — The current code prints `fmt.Fprintln(w)` after each group including the last one, causing an unnecessary trailing blank line.

Let me also re-read the test file and the other changed files.

```
   764→func TestTimeAgo(t *testing.T) {
   765→	tests := []struct {
   766→		name     string
   767→		duration time.Duration
   768→		want     string
   769→	}{
   770→		{"just now", 10 * time.Second, "just now"},
   771→		{"30 seconds", 30 * time.Second, "just now"},
   772→		{"1 minute", 1 * time.Minute, "1m ago"},
   773→		{"5 minutes", 5 * time.Minute, "5m ago"},
   774→		{"59 minutes", 59 * time.Minute, "59m ago"},
   775→		{"1 hour", 1 * time.Hour, "1h ago"},
   776→		{"3 hours", 3 * time.Hour, "3h ago"},
   777→		{"23 hours", 23 * time.Hour, "23h ago"},
   778→		{"1 day", 24 * time.Hour, "1d ago"},
   779→		{"7 days", 7 * 24 * time.Hour, "7d ago"},
   780→	}
   781→
   782→	for _, tt := range tests {
   783→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   784→			got := timeAgo(time.Now().Add(-tt.duration))
   785→			if got != tt.want {
   786→				t.Errorf("timeAgo(%v ago) = %q, want %q", tt.duration, got, tt.want)
   787→			}
   788→		})
   789→	}
   790→}
```

```
   196→// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).

   198→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) initializeSession(repo *git.Repository, sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string, userPrompt string) (*SessionState, error) {
   199→	head, err := repo.Head()
   200→	if err != nil {
   201→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   202→	}
   203→
   204→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   205→	if err != nil {
   206→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree path: %w", err)
   207→	}
   208→
   209→	// Get worktree ID for shadow branch naming
   210→	worktreeID, err := paths.GetWorktreeID(worktreePath)
   211→	if err != nil {
   212→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree ID: %w", err)
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	// Capture untracked files at session start to preserve them during rewind
   216→	untrackedFiles, err := collectUntrackedFiles()
   217→	if err != nil {
   218→		// Non-fatal: continue even if we can't collect untracked files
   219→		untrackedFiles = nil
   220→	}
   221→
   222→	state := &SessionState{
   223→		SessionID:             sessionID,
   224→		BaseCommit:            head.Hash().String(),
   225→		WorktreePath:          worktreePath,
   226→		WorktreeID:            worktreeID,
   227→		StartedAt:             time.Now(),
   228→		CheckpointCount:       0,
   229→		UntrackedFilesAtStart: untrackedFiles,
   230→		AgentType:             agentType,
   231→		TranscriptPath:        transcriptPath,
   232→		FirstPrompt:           stringutil.TruncateRunes(stringutil.CollapseWhitespace(userPrompt), 100, "..."),
   233→	}
   234→
   235→	if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   236→		return nil, err
   237→	}
   238→
   239→	return state, nil
   240→}
```

```
   900→
   901→// InitializeSession creates session state for a new session.
   902→// This is called during UserPromptSubmit hook to set up tracking for the session.
   903→// For auto-commit strategy, this creates a SessionState file in .git/entire-sessions/
   904→// to track CondensedTranscriptLines (transcript offset) across checkpoints.
   905→// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
   906→// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
   907→// userPrompt is the user's prompt text (stored truncated as FirstPrompt for display).
   908→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) InitializeSession(sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string, userPrompt string) error {
   909→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   910→	if err != nil {
   911→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   912→	}
   913→
   914→	// Get current HEAD commit to track as base
   915→	head, err := repo.Head()
   916→	if err != nil {
   917→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   918→	}
   919→
   920→	baseCommit := head.Hash().String()
   921→
   922→	// Check if session state already exists (e.g., session resuming)
   923→	existing, err := LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   924→	if err != nil {
   925→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check existing session state: %w", err)
   926→	}
   927→	if existing != nil {
   928→		// Session already initialized, nothing to do
   929→		return nil
   930→	}
   931→
   932→	// Create new session state
   933→	state := &SessionState{
   934→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   935→		BaseCommit:               baseCommit,
   936→		StartedAt:                time.Now(),
   937→		CheckpointCount:          0,
   938→		CondensedTranscriptLines: 0, // Start from beginning of transcript
   939→		FilesTouched:             []string{},
   940→		AgentType:                agentType,
   941→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptPath,
   942→		FirstPrompt:              stringutil.TruncateRunes(stringutil.CollapseWhitespace(userPrompt), 100, "..."),
   943→	}
   944→
   945→	if err := SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   946→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   947→	}
   948→
   949→	return nil
   950→}

   953→// For auto-commit, checkpoints are orphaned when no commit has an Entire-Checkpoint
   954→// trailer referencing them (e.g., after rebasing or squashing).
```

**2. AutoCommit `InitializeSession` doesn't backfill `FirstPrompt` on resume** — unlike manual-commit, it just returns early with `return nil` when `existing != nil`. This means resumed sessions on auto-commit will never get a `FirstPrompt`. Let me fix this:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01NreucnZLP12AL3MfPNkgdH
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/3/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/auto_commit.go",
  "old_string": "\tif existing != nil {\n\t\t// Session already initialized, nothing to do\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}",
  "new_string": "\tif existing != nil {\n\t\t// Session already initialized — backfill FirstPrompt if empty (for sessions\n\t\t// created before the first_prompt field was added, or resumed sessions)\n\t\tif existing.FirstPrompt == \"\" && userPrompt != \"\" {\n\t\t\texisting.FirstPrompt = stringutil.TruncateRunes(stringutil.CollapseWhitespace(userPrompt), 100, \"...\")\n\t\t\tif err := SaveSessionState(existing); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to update session state: %w\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}"
}
```

> AGENT

Wait — actually the format string is `%s%s\n` where the second `%s` is uncheckpointed. For count=1 with uncheckpointed changes, the output would be `"1 checkpoint (uncheckpointed changes)"` — no space before the parenthesis. Let me look at the format string again:

```go
fmt.Fprintf(w, "    %-9s \"%s\"  %s  %s%s\n",
    shortID, prompt, age, checkpoints, uncheckpointed)
```

For count=1, `checkpoints` = `"1 checkpoint"` and `uncheckpointed` = `" (uncheckpointed changes)"` (note the leading space). So it'd be `"1 checkpoint (uncheckpointed changes)"`. And for count=0 with uncheckpointed: `"0 checkpoints (uncheckpointed changes)"`. 

So `"1 checkpoint "` won't match because there's no space — it's immediately followed by `(uncheckpointed changes)`. Let me check the test data again: count=1, def-5678-session has PendingPromptAttribution set, so it would be `"1 checkpoint (uncheckpointed changes)"`. The pattern `"1 checkpoint "` (trailing space) won't match. But `"1 checkpoint ("` would.

Actually wait, let me reconsider. The `uncheckpointed` variable starts with a space: `" (uncheckpointed changes)"`. So the output would be `"1 checkpoint (uncheckpointed changes)"` — there IS a space between "checkpoint" and "(". So `"1 checkpoint "` will match. Good.

But also — for the 0 checkpoints case (ghi-9012), it would be `"0 checkpoints (uncheckpointed changes)"` which also contains `"1 checkpoint"` — no wait, it doesn't. OK, `"1 checkpoint "` is fine.

Actually, let me double-check: `"0 checkpoints"` doesn't contain `"1 checkpoint"`. And `"3 checkpoints"` doesn't contain `"1 checkpoint "`. `"1 checkpoint (uncheckpointed..."` does contain `"1 checkpoint "`. So the assertion is correct.

**6. The `needSave` comment in the backfill block** — The manual-commit backfill for `FirstPrompt` doesn't set `needSave = true`, but it should still save. This works because the attribution calculation below unconditionally sets `needSave = true`. But if someone later refactors the attribution to be conditional, the FirstPrompt backfill would silently stop working. This is actually the same as the lint told us — but the pattern is fragile. Let me add a comment explaining why `needSave` isn't set:

> AGENT

The duplication checker output shows pre-existing duplication in other files, not in my changes. All clean.

Here's a summary of the code quality fixes:

1. **Import ordering** — Merged the split stdlib import groups in `setup.go` into one properly sorted block
2. **Auto-commit `FirstPrompt` backfill on resume** — Added backfill logic in `AutoCommitStrategy.InitializeSession` when `existing != nil` (sessions created before the field was added would never get populated)
3. **Guard against `git -C (unknown)`** — Skip `resolveWorktreeBranch` for the `"(unknown)"` placeholder path
4. **Trailing blank line** — Changed the worktree group separator to only print between groups, not after the last one
5. **Renamed loop variable** — Changed inner loop var from `s` to `st` to avoid shadowing the receiver pattern
6. **Extracted `truncatePromptForStorage` helper** — Replaced 4 duplicated `stringutil.TruncateRunes(stringutil.CollapseWhitespace(userPrompt), 100, "...")` calls with a named helper + `maxFirstPromptRunes` constant in `manual_commit_types.go`
7. **Removed unused imports** — Cleaned up `stringutil` imports from files that now use the helper instead
8. **Extracted `unknownPlaceholder` constant** — Lint-required deduplication of `"(unknown)"` string (3 uses)
9. **Tightened test assertion** — `"1 checkpoint "` (with trailing space) instead of `"1 checkpoint"` to avoid false match against `"1 checkpoints"`
10. **Added explanatory comment** — Documented why `needSave` isn't set in the `FirstPrompt` backfill block

```
+
+// timeAgo formats a time as a human-readable relative duration.
+func timeAgo(t time.Time) string {
+	d := time.Since(t)
+	switch {
+	case d < time.Minute:
+		return "just now"
+	case d < time.Hour:
+		m := int(d.Minutes())
+		return fmt.Sprintf("%dm ago", m)
+	case d < 24*time.Hour:
+		h := int(d.Hours())
+		return fmt.Sprintf("%dh ago", h)
+	default:
+		days := int(d.Hours() / 24)
+		return fmt.Sprintf("%dd ago", days)
+	}
+}
+
+// worktreeGroup groups sessions by worktree path for display.
+type worktreeGroup struct {
+	path     string
+	branch   string
+	sessions []*session.State
+}
+
+const unknownPlaceholder = "(unknown)"
+
+// writeActiveSessions writes active session information grouped by worktree.
+func writeActiveSessions(w io.Writer) {
+	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
+	if err != nil {
+		return
+	}
+
+	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
+	if err != nil || len(states) == 0 {
+		return
+	}
+
+	// Filter to active sessions only
+	var active []*session.State
+	for _, s := range states {
+		if s.EndedAt == nil {
+			active = append(active, s)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(active) == 0 {
+		return
+	}
+
+	// Group by worktree path
+	groups := make(map[string]*worktreeGroup)
+	for _, s := range active {
+		wp := s.WorktreePath
+		if wp == "" {
+			wp = unknownPlaceholder
+		}
+		g, ok := groups[wp]
+		if !ok {
+			g = &worktreeGroup{path: wp}
+			groups[wp] = g
+		}
+		g.sessions = append(g.sessions, s)
+	}
+
+	// Resolve branch names for each worktree (skip for unknown paths)
+	for _, g := range groups {
+		if g.path != unknownPlaceholder {
+			g.branch = resolveWorktreeBranch(g.path)
+		}
+	}
+
+	// Sort groups: alphabetical by path
+	sortedGroups := make([]*worktreeGroup, 0, len(groups))
+	for _, g := range groups {
+		sortedGroups = append(sortedGroups, g)
+	}
+	sort.Slice(sortedGroups, func(i, j int) bool {
+		return sortedGroups[i].path < sortedGroups[j].path
+	})
+
+	// Sort sessions within each group by StartedAt (newest first)
+	for _, g := range sortedGroups {
+		sort.Slice(g.sessions, func(i, j int) bool {
+			return g.sessions[i].StartedAt.After(g.sessions[j].StartedAt)
+		})
+	}
+
+	fmt.Fprintln(w)
+	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Active Sessions:")
+	for i, g := range sortedGroups {
+		header := g.path
+		if g.branch != "" {
+			header += " (" + g.branch + ")"
+		}
+		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  %s\n", header)
+
+		for _, st := range g.sessions {
+			shortID := st.SessionID
+			if len(shortID) > 7 {
+				shortID = shortID[:7]
+			}
+
+			prompt := st.FirstPrompt
+			if prompt == "" {
+				prompt = unknownPlaceholder
+			}
+			prompt = stringutil.TruncateRunes(prompt, 40, "...")
+
+			age := timeAgo(st.StartedAt)
+
+			checkpoints := fmt.Sprintf("%d checkpoint", st.CheckpointCount)
+			if st.CheckpointCount != 1 {
+				checkpoints += "s"
+			}
+
+			uncheckpointed := ""
+			if st.PendingPromptAttribution != nil {
+				uncheckpointed = " (uncheckpointed changes)"
+			}
+
+			fmt.Fprintf(w, "    %-9s \"%s\"  %s  %s%s\n",
+				shortID, prompt, age, checkpoints, uncheckpointed)
+		}
+
+		// Blank line between groups, but not after the last one
+		if i < len(sortedGroups)-1 {
+			fmt.Fprintln(w)
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+// resolveWorktreeBranch resolves the current branch for a worktree path.
+func resolveWorktreeBranch(worktreePath string) string {
+	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "-C", worktreePath, "rev-parse", "--abbrev-ref", "HEAD")
+	output, err := cmd.Output()
+	if err != nil {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
+}

+func TestTimeAgo(t *testing.T) {
+	tests := []struct {
+		name     string
+		duration time.Duration
+		want     string
+	}{
+		{"just now", 10 * time.Second, "just now"},
+		{"30 seconds", 30 * time.Second, "just now"},
+		{"1 minute", 1 * time.Minute, "1m ago"},
+		{"5 minutes", 5 * time.Minute, "5m ago"},
+		{"59 minutes", 59 * time.Minute, "59m ago"},
+		{"1 hour", 1 * time.Hour, "1h ago"},
+		{"3 hours", 3 * time.Hour, "3h ago"},
+		{"23 hours", 23 * time.Hour, "23h ago"},
+		{"1 day", 24 * time.Hour, "1d ago"},
+		{"7 days", 7 * 24 * time.Hour, "7d ago"},
+	}
+
+	for _, tt := range tests {
+		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
+			got := timeAgo(time.Now().Add(-tt.duration))
+			if got != tt.want {
+				t.Errorf("timeAgo(%v ago) = %q, want %q", tt.duration, got, tt.want)
+			}
+		})
+	}
+}
+
+func TestWriteActiveSessions(t *testing.T) {
+	setupTestRepo(t)
+
+	// Create a state store with test data
+	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("NewStateStore() error = %v", err)
+	}
+
+	now := time.Now()
+
+	// Create active sessions
+	states := []*session.State{
+		{
+			SessionID:       "abc-1234-session",
+			WorktreePath:    "/Users/test/repo",
+			StartedAt:       now.Add(-2 * time.Minute),
+			CheckpointCount: 3,
+			FirstPrompt:     "Fix auth bug in login flow",
+		},
+		{
+			SessionID:       "def-5678-session",
+			WorktreePath:    "/Users/test/repo",
+			StartedAt:       now.Add(-15 * time.Minute),
+			CheckpointCount: 1,
+			FirstPrompt:     "Add dark mode support for the entire application and all components",
+			PendingPromptAttribution: &session.PromptAttribution{
+				CheckpointNumber: 2,
+			},
+		},
+		{
+			SessionID:       "ghi-9012-session",
+			WorktreePath:    "/Users/test/repo/.worktrees/3",
+			StartedAt:       now.Add(-5 * time.Minute),
+			CheckpointCount: 0,
+			PendingPromptAttribution: &session.PromptAttribution{
+				CheckpointNumber: 1,
+			},
+		},
+	}
+
+	for _, s := range states {
+		if err := store.Save(context.Background(), s); err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("Save() error = %v", err)
+		}
+	}
+
+	var buf bytes.Buffer
+	writeActiveSessions(&buf)
+
+	output := buf.String()
+
+	// Should contain "Active Sessions:" header
+	if !strings.Contains(output, "Active Sessions:") {
+		t.Errorf("Expected 'Active Sessions:' header, got: %s", output)
+	}
+
+	// Should contain worktree paths
+	if !strings.Contains(output, "/Users/test/repo") {
+		t.Errorf("Expected worktree path '/Users/test/repo', got: %s", output)
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(output, "/Users/test/repo/.worktrees/3") {
+		t.Errorf("Expected worktree path '/Users/test/repo/.worktrees/3', got: %s", output)
+	}
+
+	// Should contain truncated session IDs
+	if !strings.Contains(output, "abc-123") {
+		t.Errorf("Expected truncated session ID 'abc-123', got: %s", output)
+	}
+
+	// Should contain first prompts
+	if !strings.Contains(output, "Fix auth bug in login flow") {
+		t.Errorf("Expected first prompt text, got: %s", output)
+	}
+
+	// Should show checkpoint counts (singular and plural)
+	if !strings.Contains(output, "3 checkpoints") {
+		t.Errorf("Expected '3 checkpoints', got: %s", output)
+	}
+	// Use "1 checkpoint " (with trailing space) to avoid matching "1 checkpoints"
+	if !strings.Contains(output, "1 checkpoint ") {
+		t.Errorf("Expected '1 checkpoint ' (singular), got: %s", output)
+	}
+
+	// Should show uncheckpointed changes indicator
+	if !strings.Contains(output, "(uncheckpointed changes)") {
+		t.Errorf("Expected '(uncheckpointed changes)', got: %s", output)
+	}
+
+	// Should show "(unknown)" for session without FirstPrompt
+	if !strings.Contains(output, "(unknown)") {
+		t.Errorf("Expected '(unknown)' for missing first prompt, got: %s", output)
+	}
+}
+
+func TestWriteActiveSessions_NoSessions(t *testing.T) {
+	setupTestRepo(t)
+
+	var buf bytes.Buffer
+	writeActiveSessions(&buf)
+
+	// Should produce no output when there are no sessions
+	if buf.Len() != 0 {
+		t.Errorf("Expected empty output with no sessions, got: %s", buf.String())
+	}
+}
+
+func TestWriteActiveSessions_EndedSessionsExcluded(t *testing.T) {
+	setupTestRepo(t)
+
+	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("NewStateStore() error = %v", err)
+	}
+
+	endedAt := time.Now()
+	state := &session.State{
+		SessionID:    "ended-session",
+		WorktreePath: "/Users/test/repo",
+		StartedAt:    time.Now().Add(-10 * time.Minute),
+		EndedAt:      &endedAt,
+	}
+
+	if err := store.Save(context.Background(), state); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("Save() error = %v", err)
+	}
+
+	var buf bytes.Buffer
+	writeActiveSessions(&buf)
+
+	// Should produce no output when all sessions are ended
+	if buf.Len() != 0 {
+		t.Errorf("Expected empty output with only ended sessions, got: %s", buf.String())
+	}
+}

+	maxFirstPromptRunes = 100
 )
 
+// truncatePromptForStorage collapses whitespace and truncates a user prompt
+// for storage in FirstPrompt fields.
+func truncatePromptForStorage(prompt string) string {
+	return stringutil.TruncateRunes(stringutil.CollapseWhitespace(prompt), maxFirstPromptRunes, "...")
+}
```

> AGENT

Yes, I'd say so. `setup.go` is 1410 lines with ~30 functions covering enable, disable, status, uninstall, shell completion, hook setup, and more. The status-related code we just added (`timeAgo`, `worktreeGroup`, `writeActiveSessions`, `resolveWorktreeBranch`) is a self-contained ~140-line block that only `runStatus` calls.

Moving it to `status.go` would:
- Keep `setup.go` focused on enable/disable/uninstall/hook-setup
- Make the status logic easier to find and extend later
- Follow the pattern already used elsewhere (e.g., `commands/` has per-command files)

The natural split: `runStatus`, `runStatusDetailed`, `formatSettingsStatusShort`, `formatSettingsStatus`, `timeAgo`, `worktreeGroup`, `writeActiveSessions`, `resolveWorktreeBranch` — everything status-related. That's ~200 lines, leaving `setup.go` at ~1200 (still big, but more cohesive).

```
   453→func runStatus(w io.Writer, detailed bool) error {
   454→	// Check if we're in a git repository
   455→	if _, repoErr := paths.RepoRoot(); repoErr != nil {
   456→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✕ not a git repository")
   457→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Not being in a git repo is a valid status, not an error
   458→	}

   460→	// Get absolute paths for settings files
   461→	settingsPath, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsFile)
   462→	if err != nil {
   463→		settingsPath = EntireSettingsFile
   464→	}
   465→	localSettingsPath, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsLocalFile)
   466→	if err != nil {
   467→		localSettingsPath = EntireSettingsLocalFile
   468→	}
   469→
   470→	// Check which settings files exist
   471→	_, projectErr := os.Stat(settingsPath)
   472→	if projectErr != nil && !errors.Is(projectErr, fs.ErrNotExist) {
   473→		return fmt.Errorf("cannot access project settings file: %w", projectErr)
   474→	}
   475→	_, localErr := os.Stat(localSettingsPath)
   476→	if localErr != nil && !errors.Is(localErr, fs.ErrNotExist) {
   477→		return fmt.Errorf("cannot access local settings file: %w", localErr)
   478→	}
   479→	projectExists := projectErr == nil
   480→	localExists := localErr == nil
   481→
   482→	if !projectExists && !localExists {
   483→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "○ not set up (run `entire enable` to get started)")
   484→		return nil
   485→	}
   486→
   487→	if detailed {
   488→		return runStatusDetailed(w, settingsPath, localSettingsPath, projectExists, localExists)

   491→	// Short output: just show the effective/merged state

   506→// runStatusDetailed shows the effective status plus detailed status for each settings file.
   507→func runStatusDetailed(w io.Writer, settingsPath, localSettingsPath string, projectExists, localExists bool) error {
   508→	// First show the effective/merged status
   509→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   510→	if err != nil {
   511→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   512→	}
   513→	fmt.Fprintln(w, formatSettingsStatusShort(settings))
   514→	fmt.Fprintln(w) // blank line
   515→
   516→	// Show project settings if it exists
   517→	if projectExists {
   518→		projectSettings, err := loadSettingsFromFile(settingsPath)
   519→		if err != nil {
   520→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to load project settings: %w", err)
   521→		}
   522→		fmt.Fprintln(w, formatSettingsStatus("Project", projectSettings))
   523→	}
   524→
   525→	// Show local settings if it exists
   526→	if localExists {
   527→		localSettings, err := loadSettingsFromFile(localSettingsPath)
   528→		if err != nil {
   529→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to load local settings: %w", err)
   530→		}
   531→		fmt.Fprintln(w, formatSettingsStatus("Local", localSettings))
   532→	}
   533→
   534→	return nil
   535→}
   536→
   537→// formatSettingsStatusShort formats a short settings status line.
   538→// Output format: "Enabled (manual-commit)" or "Disabled (auto-commit)"
   539→func formatSettingsStatusShort(settings *EntireSettings) string {
   540→	displayName := settings.Strategy
   541→	if dn, ok := strategyInternalToDisplay[settings.Strategy]; ok {
   542→		displayName = dn
   543→	}
   544→
   545→	if settings.Enabled {
   546→		return fmt.Sprintf("Enabled (%s)", displayName)
   547→	}
   548→	return fmt.Sprintf("Disabled (%s)", displayName)
   549→}
   550→
   551→// formatSettingsStatus formats a settings status line with source prefix.

   553→func formatSettingsStatus(prefix string, settings *EntireSettings) string {
   554→	displayName := settings.Strategy

   557→	}

   560→		return fmt.Sprintf("%s, enabled (%s)", prefix, displayName)
   561→	}

   563→}
```
