  1270→// timeAgo formats a time as a human-readable relative duration.
  1271→func timeAgo(t time.Time) string {
  1272→	d := time.Since(t)
  1273→	switch {
  1274→	case d < time.Minute:
  1275→		return "just now"
  1276→	case d < time.Hour:
  1277→		m := int(d.Minutes())
  1278→		return fmt.Sprintf("%dm ago", m)
  1279→	case d < 24*time.Hour:
  1280→		h := int(d.Hours())
  1281→		return fmt.Sprintf("%dh ago", h)
  1282→	default:
  1283→		days := int(d.Hours() / 24)
  1284→		return fmt.Sprintf("%dd ago", days)
  1285→	}
  1286→}
  1287→
  1288→// worktreeGroup groups sessions by worktree path for display.
  1289→type worktreeGroup struct {
  1290→	path     string
  1291→	branch   string
  1292→	sessions []*session.State
  1293→}
  1294→
  1295→const unknownPlaceholder = "(unknown)"
  1296→
  1297→// writeActiveSessions writes active session information grouped by worktree.
  1298→func writeActiveSessions(w io.Writer) {
  1299→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
  1300→	if err != nil {
  1301→		return
  1302→	}
  1303→
  1304→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
  1305→	if err != nil || len(states) == 0 {
  1306→		return
  1307→	}
  1308→
  1309→	// Filter to active sessions only
  1310→	var active []*session.State
  1311→	for _, s := range states {
  1312→		if s.EndedAt == nil {
  1313→			active = append(active, s)
  1314→		}
  1315→	}
  1316→	if len(active) == 0 {
  1317→		return
  1318→	}
  1319→
  1320→	// Group by worktree path
  1321→	groups := make(map[string]*worktreeGroup)
  1322→	for _, s := range active {
  1323→		wp := s.WorktreePath
  1324→		if wp == "" {
  1325→			wp = unknownPlaceholder
  1326→		}
  1327→		g, ok := groups[wp]
  1328→		if !ok {
  1329→			g = &worktreeGroup{path: wp}
  1330→			groups[wp] = g
  1331→		}
  1332→		g.sessions = append(g.sessions, s)
  1333→	}
  1334→
  1335→	// Resolve branch names for each worktree (skip for unknown paths)
  1336→	for _, g := range groups {
  1337→		if g.path != unknownPlaceholder {
  1338→			g.branch = resolveWorktreeBranch(g.path)
  1339→		}
  1340→	}
  1341→
  1342→	// Sort groups: alphabetical by path
  1343→	sortedGroups := make([]*worktreeGroup, 0, len(groups))
  1344→	for _, g := range groups {
  1345→		sortedGroups = append(sortedGroups, g)
  1346→	}
  1347→	sort.Slice(sortedGroups, func(i, j int) bool {
  1348→		return sortedGroups[i].path < sortedGroups[j].path
  1349→	})
  1350→
  1351→	// Sort sessions within each group by StartedAt (newest first)
  1352→	for _, g := range sortedGroups {
  1353→		sort.Slice(g.sessions, func(i, j int) bool {
  1354→			return g.sessions[i].StartedAt.After(g.sessions[j].StartedAt)
  1355→		})
  1356→	}
  1357→
  1358→	fmt.Fprintln(w)
  1359→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Active Sessions:")
  1360→	for i, g := range sortedGroups {
  1361→		header := g.path
  1362→		if g.branch != "" {
  1363→			header += " (" + g.branch + ")"
  1364→		}
  1365→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  %s\n", header)
  1366→
  1367→		for _, st := range g.sessions {
  1368→			shortID := st.SessionID
  1369→			if len(shortID) > 7 {
  1370→				shortID = shortID[:7]
  1371→			}
  1372→
  1373→			prompt := st.FirstPrompt
  1374→			if prompt == "" {
  1375→				prompt = unknownPlaceholder
  1376→			}
  1377→			prompt = stringutil.TruncateRunes(prompt, 40, "...")
  1378→
  1379→			age := timeAgo(st.StartedAt)
  1380→
  1381→			checkpoints := fmt.Sprintf("%d checkpoint", st.CheckpointCount)
  1382→			if st.CheckpointCount != 1 {
  1383→				checkpoints += "s"
  1384→			}
  1385→
  1386→			uncheckpointed := ""
  1387→			if st.PendingPromptAttribution != nil {
  1388→				uncheckpointed = " (uncheckpointed changes)"
  1389→			}
  1390→
  1391→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "    %-9s \"%s\"  %s  %s%s\n",
  1392→				shortID, prompt, age, checkpoints, uncheckpointed)
  1393→		}
  1394→
  1395→		// Blank line between groups, but not after the last one
  1396→		if i < len(sortedGroups)-1 {
  1397→			fmt.Fprintln(w)
  1398→		}
  1399→	}
  1400→}
  1401→
  1402→// resolveWorktreeBranch resolves the current branch for a worktree path.
  1403→func resolveWorktreeBranch(worktreePath string) string {
  1404→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "-C", worktreePath, "rev-parse", "--abbrev-ref", "HEAD")
  1405→	output, err := cmd.Output()
  1406→	if err != nil {
  1407→		return ""
  1408→	}
  1409→	return strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
  1410→}
  1411→

(\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"io/fs\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"sort\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/stringutil\"\n\n\t\"github.com/spf13/cobra\"\n)\n\nfunc newStatusCmd() *cobra.Command {\n\tvar detailed bool\n\n\tcmd := &cobra.Command{\n\t\tUse:   \"status\",\n\t\tShort: \"Show Entire status\",\n\t\tLong:  \"Show whether Entire is currently enabled or disabled\",\n\t\tRunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {\n\t\t\treturn runStatus(cmd.OutOrStdout(), detailed)\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tcmd.Flags().BoolVar(&detailed, \"detailed\", false, \"Show detailed status for each settings file\")\n\n\treturn cmd\n}\n\nfunc runStatus(w io.Writer, detailed bool) error {\n\t// Check if we're in a git repository\n\tif _, repoErr := paths.RepoRoot(); repoErr != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(w, \"✕ not a git repository\")\n\t\treturn nil //nolint:nilerr // Not being in a git repo is a valid status, not an error\n\t}\n\n\t// Get absolute paths for settings files\n\tsettingsPath, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tsettingsPath = EntireSettingsFile\n\t}\n\tlocalSettingsPath, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsLocalFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlocalSettingsPath = EntireSettingsLocalFile\n\t}\n\n\t// Check which settings files exist\n\t_, projectErr := os.Stat(settingsPath)\n\tif projectErr != nil && !errors.Is(projectErr, fs.ErrNotExist) {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"cannot access project settings file: %w\", projectErr)\n\t}\n\t_, localErr := os.Stat(localSettingsPath)\n\tif localErr != nil && !errors.Is(localErr, fs.ErrNotExist) {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"cannot access local settings file: %w\", localErr)\n\t}\n\tprojectExists := projectErr == nil\n\tlocalExists := localErr == nil\n\n\tif !projectExists && !localExists {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(w, \"○ not set up (run `entire enable` to get started)\")\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tif detailed {\n\t\treturn runStatusDetailed(w, settingsPath, localSettingsPath, projectExists, localExists)\n\t}\n\n\t// Short output: just show the effective/merged state\n\tsettings, err := LoadEntireSettings()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to load settings: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Fprintln(w, formatSettingsStatusShort(settings))\n\n\tif settings.Enabled {\n\t\twriteActiveSessions(w)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// runStatusDetailed shows the effective status plus detailed status for each settings file.\nfunc runStatusDetailed(w io.Writer, settingsPath, localSettingsPath string, projectExists, localExists bool) error {\n\t// First show the effective/merged status\n\tsettings, err := LoadEntireSettings()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to load settings: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tfmt.Fprintln(w, formatSettingsStatusShort(settings))\n\tfmt.Fprintln(w) // blank line\n\n\t// Show project settings if it exists\n\tif projectExists {\n\t\tprojectSettings, err := loadSettingsFromFile(settingsPath)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to load project settings: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(w, formatSettingsStatus(\"Project\", projectSettings))\n\t}\n\n\t// Show local settings if it exists\n\tif localExists {\n\t\tlocalSettings, err := loadSettingsFromFile(localSettingsPath)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to load local settings: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(w, formatSettingsStatus(\"Local\", localSettings))\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// formatSettingsStatusShort formats a short settings status line.\n// Output format: \"Enabled (manual-commit)\" or \"Disabled (auto-commit)\"\nfunc formatSettingsStatusShort(settings *EntireSettings) string {\n\tdisplayName := settings.Strategy\n\tif dn, ok := strategyInternalToDisplay[settings.Strategy]; ok {\n\t\tdisplayName = dn\n\t}\n\n\tif settings.Enabled {\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"Enabled (%s)\", displayName)\n\t}\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"Disabled (%s)\", displayName)\n}\n\n// formatSettingsStatus formats a settings status line with source prefix.\n// Output format: \"Project, enabled (manual-commit)\" or \"Local, disabled (auto-commit)\"\nfunc formatSettingsStatus(prefix string, settings *EntireSettings) string {\n\tdisplayName := settings.Strategy\n\tif dn, ok := strategyInternalToDisplay[settings.Strategy]; ok {\n\t\tdisplayName = dn\n\t}\n\n\tif settings.Enabled {\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s, enabled (%s)\", prefix, displayName)\n\t}\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s, disabled (%s)\", prefix, displayName)\n}\n\n// timeAgo formats a time as a human-readable relative duration.\nfunc timeAgo(t time.Time) string {\n\td := time.Since(t)\n\tswitch {\n\tcase d < time.Minute:\n\t\treturn \"just now\"\n\tcase d < time.Hour:\n\t\tm := int(d.Minutes())\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%dm ago\", m)\n\tcase d < 24*time.Hour:\n\t\th := int(d.Hours())\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%dh ago\", h)\n\tdefault:\n\t\tdays := int(d.Hours() / 24)\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%dd ago\", days)\n\t}\n}\n\n// worktreeGroup groups sessions by worktree path for display.\ntype worktreeGroup struct {\n\tpath     string\n\tbranch   string\n\tsessions []*session.State\n}\n\nconst unknownPlaceholder = \"(unknown)\"\n\n// writeActiveSessions writes active session information grouped by worktree.\nfunc writeActiveSessions(w io.Writer) {\n\tstore, err := session.NewStateStore()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tstates, err := store.List(context.Background())\n\tif err != nil || len(states) == 0 {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Filter to active sessions only\n\tvar active []*session.State\n\tfor _, s := range states {\n\t\tif s.EndedAt == nil {\n\t\t\tactive = append(active, s)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif len(active) == 0 {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Group by worktree path\n\tgroups := make(map[string]*worktreeGroup)\n\tfor _, s := range active {\n\t\twp := s.WorktreePath\n\t\tif wp == \"\" {\n\t\t\twp = unknownPlaceholder\n\t\t}\n\t\tg, ok := groups[wp]\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tg = &worktreeGroup{path: wp}\n\t\t\tgroups[wp] = g\n\t\t}\n\t\tg.sessions = append(g.sessions, s)\n\t}\n\n\t// Resolve branch names for each worktree (skip for unknown paths)\n\tfor _, g := range groups {\n\t\tif g.path != unknownPlaceholder {\n\t\t\tg.branch = resolveWorktreeBranch(g.path)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Sort groups: alphabetical by path\n\tsortedGroups := make([]*worktreeGroup, 0, len(groups))\n\tfor _, g := range groups {\n\t\tsortedGroups = append(sortedGroups, g)\n\t}\n\tsort.Slice(sortedGroups, func(i, j int) bool {\n\t\treturn sortedGroups[i].path < sortedGroups[j].path\n\t})\n\n\t// Sort sessions within each group by StartedAt (newest first)\n\tfor _, g := range sortedGroups {\n\t\tsort.Slice(g.sessions, func(i, j int) bool {\n\t\t\treturn g.sessions[i].StartedAt.After(g.sessions[j].StartedAt)\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Fprintln(w)\n\tfmt.Fprintln(w, \"Active Sessions:\")\n\tfor i, g := range sortedGroups {\n\t\theader := g.path\n\t\tif g.branch != \"\" {\n\t\t\theader += \" (\" + g.branch + \")\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"  %s\\n\", header)\n\n\t\tfor _, st := range g.sessions {\n\t\t\tshortID := st.SessionID\n\t\t\tif len(shortID) > 7 {\n\t\t\t\tshortID = shortID[:7]\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tprompt := st.FirstPrompt\n\t\t\tif prompt == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tprompt = unknownPlaceholder\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tprompt = stringutil.TruncateRunes(prompt, 40, \"...\")\n\n\t\t\tage := timeAgo(st.StartedAt)\n\n\t\t\tcheckpoints := fmt.Sprintf(\"%d checkpoint\", st.CheckpointCount)\n\t\t\tif st.CheckpointCount != 1 {\n\t\t\t\tcheckpoints += \"s\"\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tuncheckpointed := \"\"\n\t\t\tif st.PendingPromptAttribution != nil {\n\t\t\t\tuncheckpointed = \" (uncheckpointed changes)\"\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"    %-9s \\\"%s\\\"  %s  %s%s\\n\",\n\t\t\t\tshortID, prompt, age, checkpoints, uncheckpointed)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Blank line between groups, but not after the last one\n\t\tif i < len(sortedGroups)-1 {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintln(w)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// resolveWorktreeBranch resolves the current branch for a worktree path.\nfunc resolveWorktreeBranch(worktreePath string) string {\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), \"git\", \"-C\", worktreePath, \"rev-parse\", \"--abbrev-ref\", \"HEAD\")\n\toutput, err := cmd.Output()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\treturn strings.TrimSpace(string(output))\n}\n"
}

!strings.Contains(output, \"abc-123\") {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Expected truncated session ID 'abc-123', got: %s\", output)\n\t}\n\n\t// Should contain first prompts\n\tif !strings.Contains(output, \"Fix auth bug in login flow\") {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Expected first prompt text, got: %s\", output)\n\t}\n\n\t// Should show checkpoint counts (singular and plural)\n\tif !strings.Contains(output, \"3 checkpoints\") {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Expected '3 checkpoints', got: %s\", output)\n\t}\n\t// Use \"1 checkpoint \" (with trailing space) to avoid matching \"1 checkpoints\"\n\tif !strings.Contains(output, \"1 checkpoint \") {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Expected '1 checkpoint ' (singular), got: %s\", output)\n\t}\n\n\t// Should show uncheckpointed changes indicator\n\tif !strings.Contains(output, \"(uncheckpointed changes)\") {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Expected '(uncheckpointed changes)', got: %s\", output)\n\t}\n\n\t// Should show \"(unknown)\" for session without FirstPrompt\n\tif !strings.Contains(output, \"(unknown)\") {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Expected '(unknown)' for missing first prompt, got: %s\", output)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestWriteActiveSessions_NoSessions(t *testing.T) {\n\tsetupTestRepo(t)\n\n\tvar buf bytes.Buffer\n\twriteActiveSessions(&buf)\n\n\t// Should produce no output when there are no sessions\n\tif buf.Len() != 0 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Expected empty output with no sessions, got: %s\", buf.String())\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestWriteActiveSessions_EndedSessionsExcluded(t *testing.T) {\n\tsetupTestRepo(t)\n\n\tstore, err := session.NewStateStore()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"NewStateStore() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tendedAt := time.Now()\n\tstate := &session.State{\n\t\tSessionID:    \"ended-session\",\n\t\tWorktreePath: \"/Users/test/repo\",\n\t\tStartedAt:    time.Now().Add(-10 * time.Minute),\n\t\tEndedAt:      &endedAt,\n\t}\n\n\tif err := store.Save(context.Background(), state); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Save() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tvar buf bytes.Buffer\n\twriteActiveSessions(&buf)\n\n\t// Should produce no output when all sessions are ended\n\tif buf.Len() != 0 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Expected empty output with only ended sessions, got: %s\", buf.String())\n\t}\n}\n"
}

{\n\t\tlocalSettingsPath = EntireSettingsLocalFile\n\t}\n\n\t// Check which settings files exist\n\t_, projectErr := os.Stat(settingsPath)\n\tif projectErr != nil && !errors.Is(projectErr, fs.ErrNotExist) {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"cannot access project settings file: %w\", projectErr)\n\t}\n\t_, localErr := os.Stat(localSettingsPath)\n\tif localErr != nil && !errors.Is(localErr, effective status plus detailed status for each settings file.\nfunc runStatusDetailed(w io.Writer, settingsPath, localSettingsPath string, projectExists, localExists bool) error {\n\t// First show the effective/merged status\n\tsettings, err := LoadEntireSettings()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to load settings: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tfmt.Fprintln(w, settings.Strategy\n\tif dn, ok := strategyInternalToDisplay[settings.Strategy]; ok {\n\t\tdisplayName = dn\n\t}\n\n\tif settings.Enabled {\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"Enabled (%s)\", (manual-commit)\" or \"Local, disabled (auto-commit)\"\nfunc formatSettingsStatus(prefix string, settings *EntireSettings) string {\n\tdisplayName := settings.Strategy\n\tif dn, ok := strategyInternalToDisplay[settings.Strategy]; ok {\n\t\tdisplayName = dn\n\t}\n\n\tif settings.Enabled {\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s, enabled (%s)\", prefix, displayName)\n\t}\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s, disabled (%s)\", prefix, displayName)\n}\n\n// DisabledMessage is the message shown when Entire is disabled",

  "old_string": "// timeAgo formats a time as a human-readable relative duration.\nfunc timeAgo(t time.Time) string {\n\td := time.Since(t)\n\tswitch {\n\tcase d < time.Minute:\n\t\treturn \"just now\"\n\tcase d < time.Hour:\n\t\tm := int(d.Minutes())\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%dm ago\", m)\n\tcase d < 24*time.Hour:\n\t\th := int(d.Hours())\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%dh ago\", h)\n\tdefault:\n\t\tdays := int(d.Hours() / 24)\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%dd ago\", days)\n\t}\n}\n\n// worktreeGroup groups sessions by worktree path for display.\ntype worktreeGroup struct {\n\tpath     string\n\tbranch   string\n\tsessions []*session.State\n}\n\nconst unknownPlaceholder = \"(unknown)\"\n\n// writeActiveSessions writes active session information grouped by worktree.\nfunc writeActiveSessions(w io.Writer) {\n\tstore, err := session.NewStateStore()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tstates, err := store.List(context.Background())\n\tif err != nil || len(states) == 0 {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Filter to active sessions only\n\tvar active []*session.State\n\tfor _, s := range states {\n\t\tif s.EndedAt == nil {\n\t\t\tactive = append(active, s)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif len(active) == 0 {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Group by worktree path\n\tgroups := make(map[string]*worktreeGroup)\n\tfor _, s := range active {\n\t\twp := s.WorktreePath\n\t\tif wp == \"\" {\n\t\t\twp = unknownPlaceholder\n\t\t}\n\t\tg, ok := groups[wp]\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tg = &worktreeGroup{path: wp}\n\t\t\tgroups[wp] = g\n\t\t}\n\t\tg.sessions = append(g.sessions, s)\n\t}\n\n\t// Resolve branch names for each worktree (skip for unknown paths)\n\tfor _, g := range groups {\n\t\tif g.path != unknownPlaceholder {\n\t\t\tg.branch = resolveWorktreeBranch(g.path)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Sort groups: alphabetical by path\n\tsortedGroups := make([]*worktreeGroup, 0, len(groups))\n\tfor _, g := range groups {\n\t\tsortedGroups = append(sortedGroups, g)\n\t}\n\tsort.Slice(sortedGroups, func(i, j int) bool {\n\t\treturn sortedGroups[i].path < sortedGroups[j].path\n\t})\n\n\t// Sort sessions within each group by StartedAt (newest first)\n\tfor _, g := range sortedGroups {\n\t\tsort.Slice(g.sessions, func(i, j int) bool {\n\t\t\treturn g.sessions[i].StartedAt.After(g.sessions[j].StartedAt)\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Fprintln(w)\n\tfmt.Fprintln(w, \"Active Sessions:\")\n\tfor i, g := range sortedGroups {\n\t\theader := g.path\n\t\tif g.branch != \"\" {\n\t\t\theader += \" (\" + g.branch + \")\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"  %s\\n\", header)\n\n\t\tfor _, st := range g.sessions {\n\t\t\tshortID := st.SessionID\n\t\t\tif len(shortID) > 7 {\n\t\t\t\tshortID = shortID[:7]\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tprompt := st.FirstPrompt\n\t\t\tif prompt == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tprompt = unknownPlaceholder\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tprompt = stringutil.TruncateRunes(prompt, 40, \"...\")\n\n\t\t\tage := timeAgo(st.StartedAt)\n\n\t\t\tcheckpoints := fmt.Sprintf(\"%d checkpoint\", st.CheckpointCount)\n\t\t\tif st.CheckpointCount != 1 {\n\t\t\t\tcheckpoints += \"s\"\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tuncheckpointed := \"\"\n\t\t\tif st.PendingPromptAttribution != nil {\n\t\t\t\tuncheckpointed = \" (uncheckpointed changes)\"\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"    %-9s \\\"%s\\\"  %s  %s%s\\n\",\n\t\t\t\tshortID, prompt, age, checkpoints, uncheckpointed)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Blank line between groups, but not after the last one\n\t\tif i < len(sortedGroups)-1 {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintln(w)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// resolveWorktreeBranch resolves the current branch for a worktree path.\nfunc resolveWorktreeBranch(worktreePath string) string {\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), \"git\", \"-C\", worktreePath, \"rev-parse\", \"--abbrev-ref\", \"HEAD\")\n\toutput, err := cmd.Output()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\treturn strings.TrimSpace(string(output))\n}",

> AGENT

Pushed. To your question — no, our change didn't make it unconditional. The attribution calculation on line 836-837 was already there before our PR:

```go
promptAttr := s.calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(repo, state)
state.PendingPromptAttribution = &promptAttr
needSave = true
```

That block always runs (no conditional), so `needSave` was always `true` by the time it reached the `if needSave` check. The conditional `needSave` assignments above it (AgentType backfill, transcript path update, LastCheckpointID clear) were already dead code before we touched anything.

My guess is it was written incrementally — each feature added its own `needSave = true`, and when the unconditional attribution block was added later, nobody noticed it made the earlier ones redundant. CI never flagged it because `--new-from-patch` only checks lines in the diff, and those lines weren't being touched until our PR landed nearby.

> DEVELOPER

> DEVELOPER

or is "started" better? naming is hard 😅

> AGENT

I'd go with "started" — it's more precise about what happened (a session began), whereas "opened" could imply it's still open in a terminal somewhere. Plus it pairs better with the future "last seen" since they describe different things: when it started vs when it was last active.

```
    [Claude Code] abc-123   "Fix auth bug in login..."   started 2m ago   3 checkpoints
```

Want me to go with "started"?

> AGENT

Pushed `e471ae87`.

For your question — the checkpoint count comes from `session.State.CheckpointCount` which is stored in the session state JSON file (`.git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json`). It gets incremented each time `SaveChanges` is called on the strategy — i.e., every time a checkpoint commit is made to the shadow branch. The `writeActiveSessions` function reads it directly from the state store via `store.List()`.

Perfect! Now I have all the information needed. Let me compile the findings:

## Summary

Based on my thorough examination of the codebase, here are the answers to your three questions:

### 1. **Exact Output Format `entire explain` Uses for Checkpoints**

When displaying the checkpoint list (branch view), `entire explain` groups checkpoints and shows:
- **Checkpoint header format** (line 1468 in explain.go):
  ```
  [checkpoint_id] [status_indicators] "prompt"
  ```
  Where:
  - `checkpoint_id` is truncated to 12 characters max
  - Status indicators can be `[Task]` and/or `[temporary]`
  - Prompt is quoted and truncated to 60 characters max

- **Commit entries under each checkpoint** (lines 1471-1476):
  ```
  MM-DD HH:MM (git_sha) message
  ```

- **Overall count line** (line 1316):
  ```
  Checkpoints: N
  ```

For detailed checkpoint view (`--checkpoint` flag), the format includes:
```
Checkpoint: <12-char-id>
Session: <session-id>
Created: YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS
[Author: Name <email>]  # optional for committed checkpoints
Tokens: N
[Commits: (N)
  <sha> <date> <message>]  # optional
Intent: <text>
Outcome: <text>
[Files: (N)
  - <file>]  # if verbose
[Transcript: ...]  # if verbose or full
```

### 2. **Where `PendingPromptAttribution` Gets Set and Cleared**

**SET:**
- **Lines 829-830** in `manual_commit_hooks.go`: Set when resuming a session (second+ prompt)
- **Lines 854-855** in `manual_commit_hooks.go`: Set when initializing a new session (first prompt)
- Both assignments happen in the prompt hook handler (`handlePromptHookShadow`) BEFORE the agent runs, capturing user edits since the last checkpoint

**CLEARED:**
- **Line 70** in `manual_commit_git.go`: Cleared in `SaveChanges()` after the pending attribution is moved into `PromptAttributions` array
- **Line 507** in `manual_commit_hooks.go`: Cleared when resetting/starting new sessions (error conditions)

The flow is: **Set at prompt start** (captures user baseline) → **Stored in pending state** → **Moved to PromptAttributions array when checkpoint saved** → **Cleared after use**

### 3. **Relationship Between `CheckpointCount` and Temporary Checkpoints**

**Direct relationship:**
- `CheckpointCount` is incremented at line 120 in `manual_commit_git.go`, **after** a temporary checkpoint is successfully written
- `CheckpointCount` represents the total number of completed checkpoints in the current session
- Each temporary checkpoint corresponds to exactly one increment to `CheckpointCount`

**No separate count:**
- There's **no separate counter** for temporary checkpoints on the session state
- The session state doesn't track "pending" vs "committed" checkpoint counts separately
- `CheckpointCount` is simply incremented with each successful `store.WriteTemporary()` call

**Detection of uncheckpointed changes** (what `status` command displays):
- Not based on a count, but on presence: `if st.PendingPromptAttribution != nil`
- When `PendingPromptAttribution` is non-nil, it means the user made edits at prompt start but the agent hasn't saved a checkpoint yet
- This is shown as "(uncheckpointed changes)" in the status output (line 278 in status.go)

### 4. **session.State Struct Definition**

The complete struct is defined in `/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/3/cmd/entire/cli/session/state.go` lines 24-85:

```go
type State struct {
    SessionID string                    // Unique identifier
    BaseCommit string                   // HEAD commit at session start
    WorktreePath string                 // Absolute path to worktree
    WorktreeID string                   // Git worktree identifier
    StartedAt time.Time                 // When session started
    EndedAt *time.Time                  // When session ended (nil if active)
    CheckpointCount int                 // Number of checkpoints created
    CondensedTranscriptLines int        // Lines included in previous condensation
    UntrackedFilesAtStart []string      // Files to preserve during rewind
    FilesTouched []string               // Modified/created/deleted files
    LastCheckpointID id.CheckpointID    // Checkpoint ID from last condensation
    AgentType agent.AgentType           // Agent that created session
    TokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage        // Accumulated token usage
    TranscriptLinesAtStart int          // Where checkpoint content begins
    TranscriptIdentifierAtStart string  // Session identifier for transcript
    TranscriptPath string               // Path to live transcript file
    FirstPrompt string                  // Initial user prompt (truncated)
    PromptAttributions []PromptAttribution  // Completed attribution history
    PendingPromptAttribution *PromptAttribution  // Attribution before current checkpoint
}
```

The `PromptAttribution` struct (lines 90-112) captures line-level changes:
- `CheckpointNumber`: Which checkpoint (1-indexed)
- `UserLinesAdded/Removed`: User's edits since last checkpoint
- `AgentLinesAdded/Removed`: Total agent contribution up to that point
- `UserAddedPerFile`: Per-file tracking for accurate modification detection

> AGENT

Now I see the picture. Here's what's happening:

**`entire explain`** uses `[temporary]` to mark checkpoints that haven't been committed/condensed yet. It gets the actual count from `store.ListAllTemporaryCheckpoints()` — the real git shadow branch commits.

**`entire status`** currently uses:
- `st.CheckpointCount` — incremented counter on session state (total checkpoints saved)
- `st.PendingPromptAttribution != nil` — binary flag for "uncheckpointed changes"

The problem is `status` doesn't distinguish between temporary (uncommitted) and committed checkpoints, and it doesn't show the actual temporary checkpoint count — just the total ever created.

To be consistent with `entire explain`, we should show the count of **temporary** checkpoints per session, e.g. `3 checkpoints (2 temporary)` instead of `(uncheckpointed changes)`.

This would require actually querying the shadow branch for temporary checkpoint count per session, rather than relying on the state file. Let me check if there's a simpler path — the state already tracks `CheckpointCount` and `LastCheckpointID` (set on condensation).

> TOOL

    76→	FirstPrompt string `json:"first_prompt,omitempty"`
    77→
    78→	// PromptAttributions tracks user and agent line changes at each prompt start.
    79→	// This enables accurate attribution by capturing user edits between checkpoints.
    80→	PromptAttributions []PromptAttribution `json:"prompt_attributions,omitempty"`
    81→
    82→	// PendingPromptAttribution holds attribution calculated at prompt start (before agent runs).
    83→	// This is moved to PromptAttributions when SaveChanges is called.
    84→	PendingPromptAttribution *PromptAttribution `json:"pending_prompt_attribution,omitempty"`
    85→}
    86→
    87→// PromptAttribution captures line-level attribution data at the start of each prompt.
    88→// By recording what changed since the last checkpoint BEFORE the agent works,
    89→// we can accurately separate user edits from agent contributions.
    90→type PromptAttribution struct {
    91→	// CheckpointNumber is which checkpoint this was recorded before (1-indexed)
    92→	CheckpointNumber int `json:"checkpoint_number"`
    93→
    94→	// UserLinesAdded is lines added by user since the last checkpoint
    95→	UserLinesAdded int `json:"user_lines_added"`
    96→
    97→	// UserLinesRemoved is lines removed by user since the last checkpoint
    98→	UserLinesRemoved int `json:"user_lines_removed"`
    99→
   100→	// AgentLinesAdded is total agent lines added so far (base → last checkpoint).
   101→	// Always 0 for checkpoint 1 since there's no previous checkpoint to measure against.
   102→	AgentLinesAdded int `json:"agent_lines_added"`
   103→
   104→	// AgentLinesRemoved is total agent lines removed so far (base → last checkpoint).
   105→	// Always 0 for checkpoint 1 since there's no previous checkpoint to measure against.
   106→	AgentLinesRemoved int `json:"agent_lines_removed"`
   107→
   108→	// UserAddedPerFile tracks per-file user additions for accurate modification tracking.
   109→	// This enables distinguishing user self-modifications from agent modifications.
   110→	// See docs/architecture/attribution.md for details.
   111→	UserAddedPerFile map[string]int `json:"user_added_per_file,omitempty"`
   112→}
   113→
   114→// StateStore provides low-level operations for managing session state files.
   115→//
   116→// StateStore is a primitive for session state persistence. It is NOT the same as
   117→// the Sessions interface - it only handles state files in .git/entire-sessions/,
   118→// not the full session data which includes checkpoint content.
   119→//
   120→// Use StateStore directly in strategies for performance-critical state operations.
   121→// Use the Sessions interface (when implemented) for high-level session management.
   122→type StateStore struct {
   123→	// stateDir is the directory where session state files are stored
   124→	stateDir string
   125→}
   126→
   127→// NewStateStore creates a new state store.
   128→// Uses the git common dir to store session state (shared across worktrees).
   129→func NewStateStore() (*StateStore, error) {
   130→	commonDir, err := getGitCommonDir()
   131→	if err != nil {
   132→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
   133→	}
   134→	return &StateStore{
   135→		stateDir: filepath.Join(commonDir, sessionStateDirName),
   136→	}, nil
   137→}
   138→
   139→// NewStateStoreWithDir creates a new state store with a custom directory.
   140→// This is useful for testing.
   141→func NewStateStoreWithDir(stateDir string) *StateStore {
   142→	return &StateStore{stateDir: stateDir}
   143→}
   144→
   145→// Load loads the session state for the given session ID.
   146→// Returns (nil, nil) when session file doesn't exist (not an error condition).
   147→func (s *StateStore) Load(ctx context.Context, sessionID string) (*State, error) {
   148→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   149→
   150→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   151→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   152→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   153→	}
   154→
   155→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(sessionID)
   156→
   157→	data, err := os.ReadFile(stateFile) //nolint:gosec // stateFile is derived from sessionID
   158→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   159→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil,nil indicates session not found (expected case)
   160→	}
   161→	if err != nil {
   162→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state: %w", err)
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	var state State
   166→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   167→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal session state: %w", err)
   168→	}
   169→	return &state, nil
   170→}
   171→
   172→// Save saves the session state atomically.
   173→func (s *StateStore) Save(ctx context.Context, state *State) error {
   174→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   175→
   176→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   177→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(state.SessionID); err != nil {
   178→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   179→	}
   180→
   181→	if err := os.MkdirAll(s.stateDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   182→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session state directory: %w", err)
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(state, "", "  ")
   186→	if err != nil {
   187→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal session state: %w", err)
   188→	}
   189→
   190→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(state.SessionID)
   191→
   192→	// Atomic write: write to temp file, then rename
   193→	tmpFile := stateFile + ".tmp"
   194→	if err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   195→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write session state: %w", err)
   196→	}
   197→	if err := os.Rename(tmpFile, stateFile); err != nil {
   198→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to rename session state file: %w", err)
   199→	}
   200→	return nil
   201→}
   202→
   203→// Clear removes the session state file for the given session ID.
   204→func (s *StateStore) Clear(ctx context.Context, sessionID string) error {
   205→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   206→
   207→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   208→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   209→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   210→	}
   211→
   212→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(sessionID)
   213→
   214→	if err := os.Remove(stateFile); err != nil {
   215→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   216→			return nil // Already gone, not an error
   217→		}
   218→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session state file: %w", err)
   219→	}
   220→	return nil
   221→}
   222→
   223→// RemoveAll removes the entire session state directory.
   224→// This is used during uninstall to completely remove all session state.
   225→func (s *StateStore) RemoveAll() error {
   226→	if err := os.RemoveAll(s.stateDir); err != nil {
   227→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session state directory: %w", err)
   228→	}
   229→	return nil
   230→}
   231→
   232→// List returns all session states.
   233→func (s *StateStore) List(ctx context.Context) ([]*State, error) {
   234→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   235→
   236→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(s.stateDir)
   237→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   238→		return nil, nil
   239→	}
   240→	if err != nil {
   241→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state directory: %w", err)
   242→	}
   243→
   244→	var states []*State
   245→	for _, entry := range entries {
   246→		if entry.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json") {
   247→			continue
   248→		}
   249→		if strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".tmp") {
   250→			continue // Skip temp files
   251→		}
   252→
   253→		sessionID := strings.TrimSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json")
   254→		state, err := s.Load(ctx, sessionID)
   255→		if err != nil {
   256→			continue // Skip corrupted state files
   257→		}
   258→		if state == nil {
   259→			continue
   260→		}
   261→
   262→		states = append(states, state)
   263→	}
   264→	return states, nil
   265→}
   266→
   267→// stateFilePath returns the path to a session state file.
   268→func (s *StateStore) stateFilePath(sessionID string) string {
   269→	return filepath.Join(s.stateDir, sessionID+".json")
   270→}
   271→
   272→// getGitCommonDir returns the path to the shared git directory.
   273→// In a regular checkout, this is .git/
   274→// In a worktree, this is the main repo's .git/ (not .git/worktrees/<name>/)
   275→func getGitCommonDir() (string, error) {
   276→	ctx := context.Background()
   277→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--git-common-dir")
   278→	cmd.Dir = "."
   279→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   280→	if err != nil {
   281→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
   282→	}
   283→
   284→	commonDir := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
   285→
   286→	// git rev-parse --git-common-dir returns relative paths from the working directory,
   287→	// so we need to make it absolute if it isn't already
   288→	if !filepath.IsAbs(commonDir) {
   289→		commonDir = filepath.Join(".", commonDir)
   290→	}
   291→
   292→	return filepath.Clean(commonDir), nil
   293→}
   294→

> AGENT

Here's how it currently works:

**Checkpoint count** (`status.go:271`):
- `st.CheckpointCount` — counter on `session.State`, incremented in `manual_commit_git.go:120` each time `SaveChanges` writes a temporary checkpoint

**Uncheckpointed** (`status.go:277`):
- `st.PendingPromptAttribution != nil` — set when a prompt arrives (before agent runs), cleared when `SaveChanges` completes. It's a "has the agent done work since last save?" indicator, not a count.

Neither of these tells us how many checkpoints are **temporary** (on shadow branch) vs **committed** (condensed to `entire/sessions`). `CheckpointCount` is a running total — it doesn't decrease after condensation.

To show `(X temporary)` like `entire explain` does, we have two options:

1. **Query shadow branch** — call `ListTemporaryCheckpoints` filtered by session ID to get the real count. Accurate but adds git operations per session.

2. **Track on state** — add a `CondensedCheckpointCount` field so `temporary = CheckpointCount - CondensedCheckpointCount`. Lightweight read but requires plumbing the new field through condensation.

Which approach do you prefer? Option 1 is more accurate (source of truth), option 2 is faster for status display.

> DEVELOPER

and there's the complication that sessions can span branches :|

> AGENT

Right — shadow branch migration means the base commit changes, and if the user commits mid-session, some checkpoints get condensed while the session continues. So a single session's checkpoints can be split across `entire/sessions` + a shadow branch with a different base commit than what the session started with.

That makes option 1 unreliable without knowing the full branch history, and option 2 would need to be incremented during condensation (which is doable but more plumbing).

Simplest honest approach might be to just drop the temporary/committed distinction in status and show:
- `N checkpoints` (from `CheckpointCount` — total for the session)
- Keep some indicator for unsaved work (agent is actively working but hasn't saved yet)

Or do you actually want to invest in tracking the temporary count properly?

> DEVELOPER

but checkpoint count, according to what you've said, is both temporary and persisted checkpoints?

maybe we just simplify...

What I really want to know from status is:
- what sessions do we think are "open"
- which of those sessions are going to get condensed into the next checkpoint (the answer to this may be "all of the ones in that worktree", now I say it out loud)

> AGENT

Yeah exactly — all active sessions in a worktree get condensed together on the next commit. The grouping already communicates that.

So we could simplify to just:

```
Enabled (manual-commit)

Active Sessions:
  /Users/alex/workspace/cli (main)
    [Claude Code] abc-123  "Fix auth bug in login..."   started 2m ago
    [Cursor]      def-456  "Add dark mode support..."   started 15m ago

  /Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/3 (alex/ent-64-status-shows-sessions)
    [(unknown)]   ghi-789  "Refactor status cmd..."     started 5m ago
```

Drop `CheckpointCount` and `(uncheckpointed changes)` entirely. The worktree grouping implicitly says "these all condense together on next commit." Want me to make that change?

> DEVELOPER

yeah, and then explain can show checkpoint-specific information for "code" changes
